`source/kernel/vaddress/test/vaddress_test.c`:

```c
/*
 * End-to-end check of /dev/vaddress against the host echo sub-device (0x9c08).
 * Allocates a block, backs it with host memory, sets up the stream header,
 * binds it, sends bytes, sleeps on the pin until the echo arrives and checks it.
 * Then the sync sub-device: a work item with no host sync object behind it counts as passed at once, so its fence fd
 * must become readable (signaled), and several in a row must all signal.
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0
 */
#include <errno.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <poll.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/ioctl.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include "../vaddress_uapi.h"

#define SUB_DEVICE_ECHO	0x9c08
#define HDR		0x1000
#define RING		0x1000	/* each direction */

/* Stream header layout (docs/vaddress-guest.md section 6) */
#define GUEST_READ_STATE	0x044
#define TO_HOST			0x080
#define FROM_HOST		0x100
#define R_DATA_OFFSET		0x00
#define R_SIZE			0x04
#define R_WRITE_POS		0x10
#define R_WRAP_POS		0x14
#define R_READ_POS		0x40

static volatile uint32_t *u32at(uint8_t *blk, size_t off)
{
	return (volatile uint32_t *)(blk + off);
}

#define CHECK(cond, what) do { if (!(cond)) { \
	fprintf(stderr, "FAIL: %s: %s\n", what, strerror(errno)); return 1; } \
	fprintf(stderr, "ok: %s\n", what); } while (0)

int main(void)
{
	struct va_ioctl_allocate al = { .size = HDR + 2 * RING };
	struct va_ioctl_map map = { 0 };
	struct va_ioctl_bind bind = { 0 };
	struct va_ioctl_ping ping = { 0 };
	const char msg[] = "hello from the guest";
	char got[sizeof(msg)] = { 0 };
	uint8_t *blk;
	int fd;

	fd = open("/dev/vaddress", O_RDWR | O_CLOEXEC);
	CHECK(fd >= 0, "open /dev/vaddress");
	CHECK(ioctl(fd, VA_IOCTL_ALLOCATE, &al) == 0, "ALLOCATE");
	printf("block offset 0x%llx size 0x%llx phys 0x%llx\n",
	       (unsigned long long)al.offset, (unsigned long long)al.size,
	       (unsigned long long)al.phys_addr);

	setvbuf(stdout, NULL, _IONBF, 0);
	blk = mmap(NULL, al.size, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_SHARED, fd, al.offset);
	CHECK(blk != MAP_FAILED, "mmap");
	map.offset = al.offset;
	map.size = al.size;
	CHECK(ioctl(fd, VA_IOCTL_MAP_HVA, &map) == 0, "MAP_HVA");

	memset(blk, 0, 0x180);
	fprintf(stderr, "ok: header cleared\n");
	*u32at(blk, FROM_HOST + R_DATA_OFFSET) = HDR;
	*u32at(blk, FROM_HOST + R_SIZE) = RING;
	*u32at(blk, TO_HOST + R_DATA_OFFSET) = HDR + RING;
	*u32at(blk, TO_HOST + R_SIZE) = RING;

	bind.new_offset = bind.old_offset = al.offset;
	bind.sub_device = SUB_DEVICE_ECHO;
	CHECK(ioctl(fd, VA_IOCTL_BIND, &bind) == 0, "BIND");

	/* Announce we will sleep, publish the bytes, ring, then wait. */
	*u32at(blk, GUEST_READ_STATE) = 2;
	memcpy(blk + HDR + RING, msg, sizeof(msg));
	*u32at(blk, TO_HOST + R_WRAP_POS) = sizeof(msg);
	__sync_synchronize();
	*u32at(blk, TO_HOST + R_WRITE_POS) = sizeof(msg);
	ping.offset = al.offset;
	ping.value = VA_PING_NOTIFY;
	CHECK(ioctl(fd, VA_IOCTL_PING, &ping) == 0, "PING notify");
	ping.value = VA_PING_WAIT;
	CHECK(ioctl(fd, VA_IOCTL_PING, &ping) == 0, "PING wait");

	CHECK(*u32at(blk, FROM_HOST + R_WRITE_POS) == sizeof(msg), "echo length");
	memcpy(got, blk + HDR, sizeof(msg));
	CHECK(memcmp(got, msg, sizeof(msg)) == 0, "echo bytes");
	printf("echo ok: \"%s\"\n", got);

	struct va_ioctl_offset off = { .offset = al.offset };
	munmap(blk, al.size);
	CHECK(ioctl(fd, VA_IOCTL_DEALLOCATE, &off) == 0, "DEALLOCATE");

	for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
		struct va_ioctl_sync_queue_work work = { .glsync = 0, .thread = 0 };
		struct pollfd pfd = { .events = POLLIN };

		CHECK(ioctl(fd, VA_IOCTL_SYNC_QUEUE_WORK, &work) == 0, "SYNC_QUEUE_WORK");
		pfd.fd = work.fence_fd;
		CHECK(poll(&pfd, 1, 2000) == 1 && (pfd.revents & POLLIN), "fence signaled within 2 s");
		close(work.fence_fd);
	}
	close(fd);
	puts("PASS");
	return 0;
}

```

`source/kernel/vaddress/vaddress.c`:

```c
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0
/*
 * kiln vaddress: guest driver for the vaddress address-space PCI device
 * (1c87:1074). Userland allocates blocks in BAR2, has the host back them with
 * memory, mmaps them, and binds them to host sub-devices (graphics, services)
 * that talk over byte rings inside the block. See kiln
 * docs/vaddress-guest.md for the device and ioctl protocol.
 */

#include <linux/dma-fence.h>
#include <linux/file.h>
#include <linux/interrupt.h>
#include <linux/io.h>
#include <linux/kref.h>
#include <linux/list.h>
#include <linux/miscdevice.h>
#include <linux/mm.h>
#include <linux/module.h>
#include <linux/mutex.h>
#include <linux/pci.h>
#include <linux/slab.h>
#include <linux/spinlock.h>
#include <linux/sync_file.h>
#include <linux/uaccess.h>
#include <linux/wait.h>

#include "vaddress_uapi.h"

#define VA_VENDOR_ID		0x1c87
#define VA_DEVICE_ID		0x1074

/* BAR0 registers */
#define REG_COMMAND		0x00
#define REG_STATUS		0x04
#define REG_ARG0		0x08
#define REG_ARG1		0x10
#define REG_ARG2		0x18
#define REG_ARG3		0x20
#define REG_ARG4		0x28
#define REG_DOORBELL		0x30

/* BAR1: ring of pending interrupt pins written by the host */
#define CTRL_PIN_BITS		0x00
#define CTRL_PIN_W		0x20
#define CTRL_PIN_R		0x24
#define CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN	32
#define CTRL_MIN_SIZE		0x48

enum {
	CMD_ALLOCATE_BLOCK = 0,
	CMD_DEALLOCATE_BLOCK = 1,
	CMD_MAP_HVA = 2,
	CMD_UNMAP_HVA = 3,
	CMD_BIND_SUB_DEVICE = 5,
	CMD_TERMINATE_SUB_DEVICE = 8,
	CMD_SET_PARAM = 9,
};

#define PARAM_MEM_BAR_GPA	0

/*
 * Sync sub-device: one GPU fence timeline for the whole VM (docs/vaddress.md, "Sync sub-device"). Its block holds the
 * work item the guest hands over with the doorbell, and the count of host fences passed so far, written by the host.
 * Fences are numbered in queue order and signal once the count reaches their number (goldfish sync semantics: host
 * GPU work completes in order).
 */
#define VA_SUBDEV_SYNC		0x5f01
#define SYNC_GLSYNC		0x00	/* u64, guest -> host */
#define SYNC_THREAD		0x08	/* u64, guest -> host */
#define SYNC_COMPLETED		0x10	/* u64, host -> guest */
#define SYNC_BLOCK_SIZE		PAGE_SIZE

struct va_sync {
	void *mem;			/* the bound block, NULL without a sync sub-device */
	u32 id;				/* doorbell value */
	u32 pin_mask;
	u64 context;			/* dma_fence context */
	u64 queued;			/* last fence number handed out */
	struct mutex queue_lock;	/* one work item in the block at a time */
	spinlock_t lock;		/* fence lock and pending list */
	struct list_head pending;
};

struct va_fence {
	struct dma_fence base;		/* first: dma_fence_free() frees the whole struct */
	struct list_head node;
};

struct va_dev {
	struct pci_dev *pdev;
	void __iomem *regs;
	void __iomem *ctrl;
	phys_addr_t mem_phys;
	resource_size_t mem_size;
	struct mutex regs_lock;		/* one command sequence at a time */
	bool param_sent;
	spinlock_t pins_lock;		/* bound blocks, for the IRQ handler */
	struct list_head bound;
	struct va_sync sync;
	struct miscdevice misc;
};

struct va_block {
	struct list_head node;		/* per-file list */
	struct list_head bound_node;	/* va_dev.bound, while bound */
	u64 offset;
	u64 size;
	u64 hva;			/* host cookie from MAP_HVA, 0 if unmapped */
	u64 hva_size;
	u64 hva_hint;
	u32 sub_device;			/* type id, 0 if unbound */
	u32 sub_device_id;		/* doorbell value */
	u32 pin_mask;
	wait_queue_head_t wq;
	bool signaled;			/* latched until the next wait */
	bool dead;			/* freed; wakes and fails waiters */
	struct kref ref;		/* per-file list + sleeping waiters */
};

struct va_file {
	struct va_dev *dev;
	struct mutex lock;
	struct list_head blocks;
};

static struct va_dev *va_the_dev;

/* --- register access (caller holds regs_lock) --- */

static void va_write64(struct va_dev *d, u32 off, u64 v)
{
	writel(lower_32_bits(v), d->regs + off);
	writel(upper_32_bits(v), d->regs + off + 4);
}

static u64 va_read64(struct va_dev *d, u32 off)
{
	u64 lo = readl(d->regs + off);

	return lo | ((u64)readl(d->regs + off + 4) << 32);
}

/* Returns 0 or a negative errno built from the host STATUS. */
static int va_run(struct va_dev *d, u32 cmd)
{
	u32 status;

	writel(cmd, d->regs + REG_COMMAND);
	status = readl(d->regs + REG_STATUS);
	return status ? -(int)min_t(u32, status, MAX_ERRNO) : 0;
}

/* --- sync fences --- */

static const char *va_fence_driver_name(struct dma_fence *f)
{
	return "vaddress";
}

static const char *va_fence_timeline_name(struct dma_fence *f)
{
	return "kiln-gpu";
}

static const struct dma_fence_ops va_fence_ops = {
	.get_driver_name = va_fence_driver_name,
	.get_timeline_name = va_fence_timeline_name,
};

/* Signals every pending fence the host has passed. IRQ context. */
static void va_sync_signal(struct va_sync *s)
{
	struct va_fence *f, *tmp;
	unsigned long flags;
	u64 completed = le64_to_cpu(READ_ONCE(*(__le64 *)(s->mem + SYNC_COMPLETED)));

	spin_lock_irqsave(&s->lock, flags);
	list_for_each_entry_safe(f, tmp, &s->pending, node) {
		if (f->base.seqno > completed)
			break;	/* queue order: the rest are later */
		list_del(&f->node);
		dma_fence_signal_locked(&f->base);
		dma_fence_put(&f->base);
	}
	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&s->lock, flags);
}

static long va_sync_queue_work(struct va_dev *d, void __user *argp)
{
	struct va_sync *s = &d->sync;
	struct va_ioctl_sync_queue_work a;
	struct sync_file *sf;
	struct va_fence *f;
	unsigned long flags;
	int fd;

	if (!s->mem)
		return -ENODEV;
	if (copy_from_user(&a, argp, sizeof(a)))
		return -EFAULT;
	f = kzalloc(sizeof(*f), GFP_KERNEL);
	if (!f)
		return -ENOMEM;
	fd = get_unused_fd_flags(O_CLOEXEC);
	if (fd < 0) {
		kfree(f);
		return fd;
	}

	mutex_lock(&s->queue_lock);
	dma_fence_init(&f->base, &va_fence_ops, &s->lock, s->context, ++s->queued);
	sf = sync_file_create(&f->base);	/* its own reference */
	if (!sf) {
		mutex_unlock(&s->queue_lock);
		dma_fence_put(&f->base);
		put_unused_fd(fd);
		return -ENOMEM;
	}
	/* pending before the doorbell, so the IRQ that passes it finds it; the list keeps the init reference */
	spin_lock_irqsave(&s->lock, flags);
	list_add_tail(&f->node, &s->pending);
	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&s->lock, flags);
	WRITE_ONCE(*(__le64 *)(s->mem + SYNC_GLSYNC), cpu_to_le64(a.glsync));
	WRITE_ONCE(*(__le64 *)(s->mem + SYNC_THREAD), cpu_to_le64(a.thread));
	wmb();	/* the work item before the doorbell; the host takes it before the MMIO write returns */
	writel(s->id, d->regs + REG_DOORBELL);
	mutex_unlock(&s->queue_lock);

	a.fence_fd = fd;
	if (copy_to_user(argp, &a, sizeof(a))) {
		fput(sf->file);	/* the fence stays pending and signals later; nobody waits on it */
		put_unused_fd(fd);
		return -EFAULT;
	}
	fd_install(fd, sf->file);
	return 0;
}

/* --- interrupts --- */

static irqreturn_t va_interrupt(int irq, void *data)
{
	struct va_dev *d = data;
	struct va_block *b;
	u32 w, r, n, mask = 0;
	unsigned long flags;

	w = readl(d->ctrl + CTRL_PIN_W) % CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN;
	r = readl(d->ctrl + CTRL_PIN_R) % CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN;
	n = (w - r) % CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN;
	if (!n)
		return IRQ_NONE;

	rmb();	/* entries were published before w */
	while (n--) {
		u8 pin = readb(d->ctrl + CTRL_PIN_BITS + r);

		mask |= pin < 32 ? BIT(pin) : ~0u;	/* >= 32: wake everyone */
		r = (r + 1) % CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN;
	}
	writel(r, d->ctrl + CTRL_PIN_R);

	spin_lock_irqsave(&d->pins_lock, flags);
	list_for_each_entry(b, &d->bound, bound_node) {
		if (b->pin_mask & mask) {
			b->signaled = true;
			wake_up_interruptible(&b->wq);
		}
	}
	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&d->pins_lock, flags);
	if (d->sync.pin_mask & mask)
		va_sync_signal(&d->sync);
	return IRQ_HANDLED;
}

/* --- block operations (caller holds f->lock) --- */

static struct va_block *va_find(struct va_file *f, u64 offset)
{
	struct va_block *b;

	list_for_each_entry(b, &f->blocks, node)
		if (b->offset == offset)
			return b;
	return NULL;
}

static void va_unbind_local(struct va_dev *d, struct va_block *b)
{
	unsigned long flags;

	if (!b->sub_device)
		return;
	spin_lock_irqsave(&d->pins_lock, flags);
	list_del_init(&b->bound_node);
	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&d->pins_lock, flags);
	b->sub_device = 0;
	b->sub_device_id = 0;
	b->pin_mask = 0;
}

static void va_terminate(struct va_dev *d, struct va_block *b)
{
	if (!b->sub_device)
		return;
	mutex_lock(&d->regs_lock);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG0, b->offset);
	writel(b->sub_device, d->regs + REG_ARG1);
	va_run(d, CMD_TERMINATE_SUB_DEVICE);
	mutex_unlock(&d->regs_lock);
	va_unbind_local(d, b);
}

static int va_unmap(struct va_dev *d, struct va_block *b)
{
	int ret;

	if (!b->hva_size)
		return 0;
	mutex_lock(&d->regs_lock);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG0, b->offset);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG1, b->hva);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG2, b->hva_size);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG3, b->hva_hint);
	ret = va_run(d, CMD_UNMAP_HVA);
	mutex_unlock(&d->regs_lock);
	b->hva = 0;
	b->hva_size = 0;
	return ret;
}

static void va_block_release(struct kref *ref)
{
	kfree(container_of(ref, struct va_block, ref));
}

static void va_free_block(struct va_dev *d, struct va_block *b)
{
	va_terminate(d, b);
	va_unmap(d, b);
	mutex_lock(&d->regs_lock);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG0, b->offset);
	va_run(d, CMD_DEALLOCATE_BLOCK);
	mutex_unlock(&d->regs_lock);
	list_del(&b->node);
	WRITE_ONCE(b->dead, true);
	wake_up_interruptible_all(&b->wq);
	kref_put(&b->ref, va_block_release);
}

static long va_allocate(struct va_file *f, void __user *argp)
{
	struct va_dev *d = f->dev;
	struct va_ioctl_allocate a;
	struct va_block *b;
	int ret;

	if (copy_from_user(&a, argp, sizeof(a)))
		return -EFAULT;
	b = kzalloc(sizeof(*b), GFP_KERNEL);
	if (!b)
		return -ENOMEM;

	mutex_lock(&d->regs_lock);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG0, a.size);
	ret = va_run(d, CMD_ALLOCATE_BLOCK);
	if (!ret) {
		b->size = va_read64(d, REG_ARG0);
		b->offset = va_read64(d, REG_ARG1);
	}
	mutex_unlock(&d->regs_lock);
	if (ret) {
		kfree(b);
		return ret;
	}

	INIT_LIST_HEAD(&b->bound_node);
	init_waitqueue_head(&b->wq);
	kref_init(&b->ref);
	list_add(&b->node, &f->blocks);

	a.size = b->size;
	a.offset = b->offset;
	a.phys_addr = d->mem_phys + b->offset;
	if (copy_to_user(argp, &a, sizeof(a))) {
		va_free_block(d, b);
		return -EFAULT;
	}
	return 0;
}

static long va_deallocate(struct va_file *f, void __user *argp)
{
	struct va_ioctl_offset a;
	struct va_block *b;

	if (copy_from_user(&a, argp, sizeof(a)))
		return -EFAULT;
	b = va_find(f, a.offset);
	if (!b)
		return -EINVAL;
	va_free_block(f->dev, b);
	return 0;
}

static long va_map_hva(struct va_file *f, void __user *argp)
{
	struct va_dev *d = f->dev;
	struct va_ioctl_map a;
	struct va_block *b;
	int ret;

	if (copy_from_user(&a, argp, sizeof(a)))
		return -EFAULT;
	b = va_find(f, a.offset);
	if (!b)
		return -EINVAL;
	if (b->hva_size)
		return -EEXIST;
	if (!a.size || a.size > b->size)
		return -EINVAL;

	mutex_lock(&d->regs_lock);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG0, b->offset);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG1, a.hva);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG2, a.size);
	ret = va_run(d, CMD_MAP_HVA);
	if (!ret) {
		b->hva = va_read64(d, REG_ARG1);
		b->hva_size = va_read64(d, REG_ARG2);
		b->hva_hint = a.hva;
	}
	mutex_unlock(&d->regs_lock);
	if (ret)
		return ret;

	a.hva = b->hva;
	a.size = b->hva_size;
	if (copy_to_user(argp, &a, sizeof(a))) {
		va_unmap(d, b);
		return -EFAULT;
	}
	return 0;
}

static long va_unmap_hva(struct va_file *f, void __user *argp)
{
	struct va_ioctl_offset a;
	struct va_block *b;

	if (copy_from_user(&a, argp, sizeof(a)))
		return -EFAULT;
	b = va_find(f, a.offset);
	if (!b || !b->hva_size)
		return -EINVAL;
	va_terminate(f->dev, b);
	return va_unmap(f->dev, b);
}

static long va_bind(struct va_file *f, void __user *argp)
{
	struct va_dev *d = f->dev;
	struct va_ioctl_bind a;
	struct va_block *nb, *ob = NULL;
	unsigned long flags;
	int ret;

	if (copy_from_user(&a, argp, sizeof(a)))
		return -EFAULT;
	nb = va_find(f, a.new_offset);
	if (!nb || !nb->hva_size || nb->sub_device || a.sub_device <= 0)
		return -EFAULT;
	if (a.old_offset != a.new_offset) {
		/* moving an instance to a bigger block (ring growth) */
		ob = va_find(f, a.old_offset);
		if (!ob || !ob->sub_device || ob->sub_device != a.sub_device)
			return -EINVAL;
	}

	mutex_lock(&d->regs_lock);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG0, nb->offset);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG1, nb->hva);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG2, nb->size);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG3, ob ? ob->offset : nb->offset);
	writel(a.sub_device, d->regs + REG_ARG4);
	ret = va_run(d, CMD_BIND_SUB_DEVICE);
	if (!ret) {
		nb->sub_device_id = readl(d->regs + REG_ARG0);
		nb->pin_mask = readl(d->regs + REG_ARG1);
	}
	mutex_unlock(&d->regs_lock);
	if (ret)
		return ret;

	if (ob)
		va_unbind_local(d, ob);	/* the host moved it; no TERMINATE */
	nb->sub_device = a.sub_device;
	nb->signaled = false;
	spin_lock_irqsave(&d->pins_lock, flags);
	list_add(&nb->bound_node, &d->bound);
	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&d->pins_lock, flags);
	return 0;
}

/* PING: value VA_PING_WAIT sleeps until the block's pin fires; anything else
 * rings the doorbell. Called without f->lock held while sleeping. */
static long va_ping(struct va_file *f, void __user *argp)
{
	struct va_dev *d = f->dev;
	struct va_ioctl_ping a;
	struct va_block *b;
	u32 id;
	long ret;

	if (copy_from_user(&a, argp, sizeof(a)))
		return -EFAULT;

	mutex_lock(&f->lock);
	b = va_find(f, a.offset);
	if (!b || !b->hva_size || !b->sub_device) {
		mutex_unlock(&f->lock);
		return -EFAULT;
	}
	if (a.value != VA_PING_WAIT) {
		id = b->sub_device_id;
		mutex_unlock(&f->lock);
		writel(id, d->regs + REG_DOORBELL);
		return 0;
	}
	/* Another thread may free the block while we sleep: hold a ref. */
	kref_get(&b->ref);
	mutex_unlock(&f->lock);

	ret = wait_event_interruptible(b->wq, READ_ONCE(b->signaled) ||
				       READ_ONCE(b->dead));
	/* Only a completed wait consumes the latch: after -ERESTARTSYS an IRQ that landed since the check must stay for the retry. */
	if (!ret)
		WRITE_ONCE(b->signaled, false);
	if (!ret && READ_ONCE(b->dead))
		ret = -EFAULT;
	kref_put(&b->ref, va_block_release);
	return ret;
}

static long va_ioctl(struct file *filp, unsigned int cmd, unsigned long arg)
{
	struct va_file *f = filp->private_data;
	void __user *argp = (void __user *)arg;
	long ret;

	if (cmd == VA_IOCTL_PING)
		return va_ping(f, argp);
	if (cmd == VA_IOCTL_SYNC_QUEUE_WORK)
		return va_sync_queue_work(f->dev, argp);

	mutex_lock(&f->lock);
	switch (cmd) {
	case VA_IOCTL_ALLOCATE:
		ret = va_allocate(f, argp);
		break;
	case VA_IOCTL_DEALLOCATE:
		ret = va_deallocate(f, argp);
		break;
	case VA_IOCTL_MAP_HVA:
		ret = va_map_hva(f, argp);
		break;
	case VA_IOCTL_UNMAP_HVA:
		ret = va_unmap_hva(f, argp);
		break;
	case VA_IOCTL_BIND:
		ret = va_bind(f, argp);
		break;
	default:
		ret = -ENOTTY;
	}
	mutex_unlock(&f->lock);
	return ret;
}

static int va_mmap(struct file *filp, struct vm_area_struct *vma)
{
	struct va_file *f = filp->private_data;
	u64 off = (u64)vma->vm_pgoff << PAGE_SHIFT;
	u64 len = vma->vm_end - vma->vm_start;
	struct va_block *b;
	int ret = -EINVAL;

	mutex_lock(&f->lock);
	list_for_each_entry(b, &f->blocks, node) {
		if (off >= b->offset && off + len <= b->offset + b->size) {
			/* Host RAM appears at this BAR2 range once MAP_HVA runs. */
			ret = remap_pfn_range(vma, vma->vm_start,
					      (f->dev->mem_phys + off) >> PAGE_SHIFT,
					      len, vma->vm_page_prot);
			break;
		}
	}
	mutex_unlock(&f->lock);
	return ret;
}

/* The host needs BAR2's guest-physical address before any block command. */
static int va_send_param(struct va_dev *d)
{
	int ret = 0;

	mutex_lock(&d->regs_lock);
	if (!d->param_sent) {
		writel(PARAM_MEM_BAR_GPA, d->regs + REG_ARG0);
		va_write64(d, REG_ARG0 + 4, d->mem_phys);
		ret = va_run(d, CMD_SET_PARAM);
		d->param_sent = !ret;
	}
	mutex_unlock(&d->regs_lock);
	return ret;
}

/*
 * Binds the VM's sync sub-device on a host-backed block of its own. A host without one (ENODEV) leaves sync off: the
 * guest GL then is not offered native sync and keeps its glFinish fallback.
 */
static void va_sync_setup(struct va_dev *d)
{
	struct va_sync *s = &d->sync;
	u64 offset, size, hva;
	u32 pin_mask = 0;
	int ret;

	mutex_init(&s->queue_lock);
	spin_lock_init(&s->lock);
	INIT_LIST_HEAD(&s->pending);
	s->context = dma_fence_context_alloc(1);
	if (va_send_param(d))
		return;

	mutex_lock(&d->regs_lock);
	va_write64(d, REG_ARG0, SYNC_BLOCK_SIZE);
	ret = va_run(d, CMD_ALLOCATE_BLOCK);
	size = va_read64(d, REG_ARG0);
	offset = va_read64(d, REG_ARG1);
	if (!ret) {
		va_write64(d, REG_ARG0, offset);
		va_write64(d, REG_ARG1, 0);	/* the host allocates */
		va_write64(d, REG_ARG2, size);
		ret = va_run(d, CMD_MAP_HVA);
		hva = va_read64(d, REG_ARG1);
	}
	if (!ret) {
		va_write64(d, REG_ARG0, offset);
		va_write64(d, REG_ARG1, hva);
		va_write64(d, REG_ARG2, size);
		va_write64(d, REG_ARG3, offset);
		writel(VA_SUBDEV_SYNC, d->regs + REG_ARG4);
		ret = va_run(d, CMD_BIND_SUB_DEVICE);
		s->id = readl(d->regs + REG_ARG0);
		pin_mask = readl(d->regs + REG_ARG1);
	}
	mutex_unlock(&d->regs_lock);
	if (ret) {
		/* ponytail: the block stays allocated (one page per boot on an old host); free it if hosts get picky */
		dev_info(&d->pdev->dev, "no sync sub-device (%d): GPU fences off\n", ret);
		return;
	}
	s->mem = devm_memremap(&d->pdev->dev, d->mem_phys + offset, size, MEMREMAP_WB);
	if (IS_ERR(s->mem)) {
		dev_warn(&d->pdev->dev, "sync block not mappable (%ld): GPU fences off\n", PTR_ERR(s->mem));
		s->mem = NULL;
		return;
	}
	WRITE_ONCE(s->pin_mask, pin_mask);	/* last: the IRQ handler looks at the block once this is set */
	dev_info(&d->pdev->dev, "sync sub-device %u bound: GPU fences on\n", s->id);
}

static int va_open(struct inode *inode, struct file *filp)
{
	struct va_dev *d = va_the_dev;
	struct va_file *f;

	if (!d)
		return -ENODEV;
	if (va_send_param(d))
		return -EINVAL;

	f = kzalloc(sizeof(*f), GFP_KERNEL);
	if (!f)
		return -ENOMEM;
	f->dev = d;
	mutex_init(&f->lock);
	INIT_LIST_HEAD(&f->blocks);
	filp->private_data = f;
	return 0;
}

static int va_release(struct inode *inode, struct file *filp)
{
	struct va_file *f = filp->private_data;
	struct va_block *b, *tmp;

	mutex_lock(&f->lock);
	list_for_each_entry_safe(b, tmp, &f->blocks, node)
		va_free_block(f->dev, b);
	mutex_unlock(&f->lock);
	kfree(f);
	return 0;
}

static const struct file_operations va_fops = {
	.owner = THIS_MODULE,
	.open = va_open,
	.release = va_release,
	.unlocked_ioctl = va_ioctl,
	.compat_ioctl = compat_ptr_ioctl,
	.mmap = va_mmap,
};

static int va_probe(struct pci_dev *pdev, const struct pci_device_id *id)
{
	struct va_dev *d;
	int ret;

	if (pdev->revision != 0)
		return -ENODEV;
	if (va_the_dev)
		return -EBUSY;	/* one device per VM */

	d = devm_kzalloc(&pdev->dev, sizeof(*d), GFP_KERNEL);
	if (!d)
		return -ENOMEM;
	d->pdev = pdev;
	mutex_init(&d->regs_lock);
	spin_lock_init(&d->pins_lock);
	INIT_LIST_HEAD(&d->bound);

	ret = pcim_enable_device(pdev);
	if (ret)
		return ret;
	/* MSI messages are bus-master writes: without this the host drops them. */
	pci_set_master(pdev);
	ret = dma_set_mask_and_coherent(&pdev->dev, DMA_BIT_MASK(64));
	if (ret)
		return ret;
	if (!(pci_resource_flags(pdev, 1) & IORESOURCE_MEM) ||
	    pci_resource_len(pdev, 1) < CTRL_MIN_SIZE)
		return -ENODEV;
	ret = pcim_iomap_regions(pdev, BIT(0) | BIT(1), "vaddress");
	if (ret)
		return ret;
	d->regs = pcim_iomap_table(pdev)[0];
	d->ctrl = pcim_iomap_table(pdev)[1];
	d->mem_phys = pci_resource_start(pdev, 2);
	d->mem_size = pci_resource_len(pdev, 2);

	/* Device-managed via pcim_enable_device(), like the devm IRQ below. */
	ret = pci_alloc_irq_vectors(pdev, 1, 1, PCI_IRQ_MSI | PCI_IRQ_INTX);
	if (ret < 0)
		return ret;
	ret = devm_request_irq(&pdev->dev, pci_irq_vector(pdev, 0), va_interrupt,
			       IRQF_SHARED, "vaddress", d);
	if (ret)
		return ret;

	d->misc.minor = MISC_DYNAMIC_MINOR;
	d->misc.name = "vaddress";
	d->misc.fops = &va_fops;
	d->misc.mode = 0666;
	ret = misc_register(&d->misc);
	if (ret)
		return ret;

	pci_set_drvdata(pdev, d);
	va_sync_setup(d);	/* before the device is visible, so no GL process starts without it */
	va_the_dev = d;
	dev_info(&pdev->dev, "BAR2 %pa (%llu MiB)\n", &d->mem_phys,
		 (unsigned long long)d->mem_size >> 20);
	return 0;
}

static void va_remove(struct pci_dev *pdev)
{
	struct va_dev *d = pci_get_drvdata(pdev);

	misc_deregister(&d->misc);
	va_the_dev = NULL;
}

static const struct pci_device_id va_ids[] = {
	{ PCI_DEVICE(VA_VENDOR_ID, VA_DEVICE_ID) },
	{ }
};
MODULE_DEVICE_TABLE(pci, va_ids);

static struct pci_driver va_driver = {
	.name = "vaddress",
	.id_table = va_ids,
	.probe = va_probe,
	.remove = va_remove,
};
module_pci_driver(va_driver);

MODULE_DESCRIPTION("kiln vaddress address-space device");
MODULE_LICENSE("GPL");

```

`source/kernel/vaddress/vaddress_uapi.h`:

```h
/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0 WITH Linux-syscall-note */
/*
 * kiln /dev/vaddress ioctl interface (shared with guest userland).
 */
#ifndef _UAPI_VADDRESS_H
#define _UAPI_VADDRESS_H

#include <linux/ioctl.h>
#include <linux/types.h>

struct va_ioctl_allocate {
	__u64 size;		/* in: requested; out: host-rounded */
	__u64 offset;		/* out: offset in the mmap space */
	__u64 phys_addr;	/* out: guest-physical address */
};

struct va_ioctl_offset {
	__u64 offset;
};

struct va_ioctl_map {
	__u64 offset;
	__u64 hva;		/* in: 0 = host allocates; out: host cookie */
	__u64 size;		/* in: requested; out: mapped */
};

#define VA_PING_WAIT		0x76	/* sleep until the block's pin fires */
#define VA_PING_NOTIFY		0x86	/* ring the doorbell */

struct va_ioctl_ping {
	__u64 offset;
	__u32 value;
	__u32 pad;
};

struct va_ioctl_bind {
	__u64 new_offset;
	__u64 old_offset;	/* == new_offset when there is no old block */
	__s32 sub_device;	/* host sub-device type id */
	__s32 pad;
};

#define VA_IOCTL_ALLOCATE	_IOWR('L', 0x15, struct va_ioctl_allocate)
#define VA_IOCTL_DEALLOCATE	_IOWR('L', 0x16, struct va_ioctl_offset)
#define VA_IOCTL_MAP_HVA	_IOWR('L', 0x17, struct va_ioctl_map)
#define VA_IOCTL_UNMAP_HVA	_IOWR('L', 0x18, struct va_ioctl_offset)
#define VA_IOCTL_PING		_IOWR('L', 0x19, struct va_ioctl_ping)
/* A sync_file fd that signals when the host GPU passes a host sync object (gfxstream's goldfish_sync_queue_work:
 * glsync and thread as rcCreateSyncKHR returned them). ENODEV when the host has no sync sub-device. */
struct va_ioctl_sync_queue_work {
	__u64 glsync;
	__u64 thread;
	__s32 fence_fd;		/* out */
	__s32 pad;
};

#define VA_IOCTL_BIND		_IOWR('L', 0x1a, struct va_ioctl_bind)
#define VA_IOCTL_SYNC_QUEUE_WORK _IOWR('L', 0x1b, struct va_ioctl_sync_queue_work)

#endif

```

`source/kernel/virt_wifi/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/bash
# Builds virt_wifi.ko (with the ssid=/bssid= parameters) for the guest kernel: LineageOS android_kernel_virt_virtio at
# 4f6bf47200d9, the guest's own config, release 6.12.81-4k-g4f6bf47200d9. Runs in the kiln-kernel-build image of the
# colima profile "openmumu"; the kernel tree lives in the Docker volume kiln-kernel-src (a case-sensitive file system:
# on the Mac's disk, files like xt_CONNMARK.h / xt_connmark.h overwrite each other).
# Needs, in $KILN_KERNEL (default /Volumes/Expansion/Users/nks1608/Developer/kiln-kernel): config.gz and vmlinux.symvers
# from the guest kernel, and refmods/modules/virt_wifi.ko (the image's own) to check the symbol versions against.
# Output: $KILN_KERNEL/virt_wifi.ko
set -euo pipefail
S="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
KK="${KILN_KERNEL:-/Volumes/Expansion/Users/nks1608/Developer/kiln-kernel}"
export DOCKER_HOST="${DOCKER_HOST:-unix:///Volumes/Expansion/Users/nks1608/.colima/openmumu/docker.sock}"
mkdir -p "$KK/virt_wifi-src"
cp "$S/virt_wifi.c" "$S/Kbuild" "$KK/virt_wifi-src/"
docker volume create kiln-kernel-src >/dev/null
docker run --rm -v kiln-kernel-src:/k -v "$KK":/kk kiln-kernel-build bash -c '
set -euo pipefail
T=/k/tree M="make -s ARCH=arm64 LLVM=1"
if [ ! -d $T/.git ]; then
  mkdir -p $T && cd $T && git init -q
  git fetch -q --depth 1 https://github.com/LineageOS/android_kernel_virt_virtio 4f6bf47200d9cf735e65cad80823295d2141982c
  git checkout -q FETCH_HEAD
fi
cd $T
if [ ! -f Module.symvers ] || ! cmp -s <(gzip -dc /kk/config.gz) .config.kiln; then
  gzip -dc /kk/config.gz > .config; cp .config .config.kiln
  $M olddefconfig; $M -j"$(nproc)" modules_prepare
  cp /kk/vmlinux.symvers Module.symvers
fi
rel=$($M kernelrelease); [ "$rel" = 6.12.81-4k-g4f6bf47200d9 ] || { echo "kernel release is $rel" >&2; exit 1; }
# cfg80211 for the versions of the symbols virt_wifi uses (it needs rfkill, another module: its own imports only warn)
[ -s net/wireless/Module.symvers ] || $M -j"$(nproc)" M=net/wireless KBUILD_MODPOST_WARN=1 modules
rm -rf /k/virt_wifi && cp -r /kk/virt_wifi-src /k/virt_wifi
$M -C $T M=/k/virt_wifi KBUILD_EXTRA_SYMBOLS=$T/net/wireless/Module.symvers modules
llvm-strip --strip-debug /k/virt_wifi/virt_wifi.ko
# the symbol versions must be the ones the image'"'"'s cfg80211 and kernel export, or the module is refused
python3 - /kk/refmods/modules/virt_wifi.ko /k/virt_wifi/virt_wifi.ko <<PY
import sys, struct
def versions(path):
    data = open(path, "rb").read()
    import subprocess
    subprocess.run(["llvm-objcopy", "-O", "binary", "--only-section=__versions", path, "/tmp/versions.bin"], check=True)
    out = open("/tmp/versions.bin", "rb").read()
    v = {}
    for i in range(0, len(out), 64):
        crc = struct.unpack_from("<Q", out, i)[0]; name = out[i+8:i+64].split(b"\0")[0].decode()  # u64 crc, char[56]
        if name: v[name] = crc
    return v
ref, ours = versions(sys.argv[1]), versions(sys.argv[2])
bad = [n for n in ours if n in ref and ours[n] != ref[n]]
missing = [n for n in ours if n not in ref]
print(f"symbol versions: {len(ours)} used, {len(bad)} differ from the image, {len(missing)} not in the original module")
if bad or not ours: print("differ:", bad[:10] or "no __versions read"); sys.exit(1)
PY
modinfo /k/virt_wifi/virt_wifi.ko | grep -E "^(vermagic|parm):"
cp /k/virt_wifi/virt_wifi.ko /kk/virt_wifi.ko
'
echo "built: $KK/virt_wifi.ko"

```

`source/kernel/virt_wifi/virt_wifi.c`:

```c
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0
/* drivers/net/wireless/virt_wifi.c
 *
 * A fake implementation of cfg80211_ops that can be tacked on to an ethernet
 * net_device to make it appear as a wireless connection.
 *
 * Copyright (C) 2018 Google, Inc.
 *
 * Author: schuffelen@google.com
 */

#include <net/cfg80211.h>
#include <net/rtnetlink.h>
#include <linux/etherdevice.h>
#include <linux/math64.h>
#include <linux/module.h>

static struct wiphy *common_wiphy;

struct virt_wifi_wiphy_priv {
	struct delayed_work scan_result;
	struct cfg80211_scan_request *scan_request;
	bool being_deleted;
};

static struct ieee80211_channel channel_2ghz = {
	.band = NL80211_BAND_2GHZ,
	.center_freq = 2432,
	.hw_value = 2432,
	.max_power = 20,
};

static struct ieee80211_rate bitrates_2ghz[] = {
	{ .bitrate = 10 },
	{ .bitrate = 20 },
	{ .bitrate = 55 },
	{ .bitrate = 110 },
	{ .bitrate = 60 },
	{ .bitrate = 120 },
	{ .bitrate = 240 },
};

static struct ieee80211_supported_band band_2ghz = {
	.channels = &channel_2ghz,
	.bitrates = bitrates_2ghz,
	.band = NL80211_BAND_2GHZ,
	.n_channels = 1,
	.n_bitrates = ARRAY_SIZE(bitrates_2ghz),
	.ht_cap = {
		.ht_supported = true,
		.cap = IEEE80211_HT_CAP_SUP_WIDTH_20_40 |
		       IEEE80211_HT_CAP_GRN_FLD |
		       IEEE80211_HT_CAP_SGI_20 |
		       IEEE80211_HT_CAP_SGI_40 |
		       IEEE80211_HT_CAP_DSSSCCK40,
		.ampdu_factor = 0x3,
		.ampdu_density = 0x6,
		.mcs = {
			.rx_mask = {0xff, 0xff},
			.tx_params = IEEE80211_HT_MCS_TX_DEFINED,
		},
	},
};

static struct ieee80211_channel channel_5ghz = {
	.band = NL80211_BAND_5GHZ,
	.center_freq = 5240,
	.hw_value = 5240,
	.max_power = 20,
};

static struct ieee80211_rate bitrates_5ghz[] = {
	{ .bitrate = 60 },
	{ .bitrate = 120 },
	{ .bitrate = 240 },
};

#define RX_MCS_MAP (IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 0 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 2 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 4 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 6 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 8 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 10 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 12 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 14)

#define TX_MCS_MAP (IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 0 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 2 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 4 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 6 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 8 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 10 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 12 | \
		    IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9 << 14)

static struct ieee80211_supported_band band_5ghz = {
	.channels = &channel_5ghz,
	.bitrates = bitrates_5ghz,
	.band = NL80211_BAND_5GHZ,
	.n_channels = 1,
	.n_bitrates = ARRAY_SIZE(bitrates_5ghz),
	.ht_cap = {
		.ht_supported = true,
		.cap = IEEE80211_HT_CAP_SUP_WIDTH_20_40 |
		       IEEE80211_HT_CAP_GRN_FLD |
		       IEEE80211_HT_CAP_SGI_20 |
		       IEEE80211_HT_CAP_SGI_40 |
		       IEEE80211_HT_CAP_DSSSCCK40,
		.ampdu_factor = 0x3,
		.ampdu_density = 0x6,
		.mcs = {
			.rx_mask = {0xff, 0xff},
			.tx_params = IEEE80211_HT_MCS_TX_DEFINED,
		},
	},
	.vht_cap = {
		.vht_supported = true,
		.cap = IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_MAX_MPDU_LENGTH_11454 |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_SUPP_CHAN_WIDTH_160_80PLUS80MHZ |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_RXLDPC |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_SHORT_GI_80 |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_SHORT_GI_160 |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_TXSTBC |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_RXSTBC_1 |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_RXSTBC_2 |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_RXSTBC_3 |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_RXSTBC_4 |
		       IEEE80211_VHT_CAP_MAX_A_MPDU_LENGTH_EXPONENT_MASK,
		.vht_mcs = {
			.rx_mcs_map = cpu_to_le16(RX_MCS_MAP),
			.tx_mcs_map = cpu_to_le16(TX_MCS_MAP),
		}
	},
};

/* Assigned at module init: the `bssid` parameter, or random (locally-administered and unicast). */
static u8 fake_router_bssid[ETH_ALEN] __ro_after_init = {};

/*
 * Kiln: the network the fake access point offers is a module parameter instead of the fixed "VirtWifi", so each
 * instance can show its own (passed on the kernel command line as virt_wifi.ssid=... and virt_wifi.bssid=..., which
 * Android's module loader hands to the module). Both are readable in /sys/module/virt_wifi/parameters.
 */
static char *ssid = "VirtWifi";
module_param(ssid, charp, 0444);
MODULE_PARM_DESC(ssid, "network name of the fake access point (1 to 32 bytes)");
static char *bssid = "";
module_param(bssid, charp, 0444);
MODULE_PARM_DESC(bssid, "MAC address of the fake access point (aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff); random when empty");

static u8 virt_wifi_ssid[IEEE80211_MAX_SSID_LEN] __ro_after_init;
static u8 virt_wifi_ssid_len __ro_after_init;

static void virt_wifi_inform_bss(struct wiphy *wiphy)
{
	u64 tsf = div_u64(ktime_get_boottime_ns(), 1000);
	struct cfg80211_bss *informed_bss;
	u8 ie[2 + IEEE80211_MAX_SSID_LEN];

	ie[0] = WLAN_EID_SSID;
	ie[1] = virt_wifi_ssid_len;
	memcpy(ie + 2, virt_wifi_ssid, virt_wifi_ssid_len);
	informed_bss = cfg80211_inform_bss(wiphy, &channel_5ghz,
					   CFG80211_BSS_FTYPE_PRESP,
					   fake_router_bssid, tsf,
					   WLAN_CAPABILITY_ESS, 0,
					   ie, 2 + virt_wifi_ssid_len,
					   DBM_TO_MBM(-50), GFP_KERNEL);
	cfg80211_put_bss(wiphy, informed_bss);
}

/* Called with the rtnl lock held. */
static int virt_wifi_scan(struct wiphy *wiphy,
			  struct cfg80211_scan_request *request)
{
	struct virt_wifi_wiphy_priv *priv = wiphy_priv(wiphy);

	wiphy_debug(wiphy, "scan\n");

	if (priv->scan_request || priv->being_deleted)
		return -EBUSY;

	priv->scan_request = request;
	schedule_delayed_work(&priv->scan_result, HZ * 2);

	return 0;
}

/* Acquires and releases the rdev BSS lock. */
static void virt_wifi_scan_result(struct work_struct *work)
{
	struct virt_wifi_wiphy_priv *priv =
		container_of(work, struct virt_wifi_wiphy_priv,
			     scan_result.work);
	struct wiphy *wiphy = priv_to_wiphy(priv);
	struct cfg80211_scan_info scan_info = { .aborted = false };

	virt_wifi_inform_bss(wiphy);

	/* Schedules work which acquires and releases the rtnl lock. */
	cfg80211_scan_done(priv->scan_request, &scan_info);
	priv->scan_request = NULL;
}

/* May acquire and release the rdev BSS lock. */
static void virt_wifi_cancel_scan(struct wiphy *wiphy)
{
	struct virt_wifi_wiphy_priv *priv = wiphy_priv(wiphy);

	cancel_delayed_work_sync(&priv->scan_result);
	/* Clean up dangling callbacks if necessary. */
	if (priv->scan_request) {
		struct cfg80211_scan_info scan_info = { .aborted = true };
		/* Schedules work which acquires and releases the rtnl lock. */
		cfg80211_scan_done(priv->scan_request, &scan_info);
		priv->scan_request = NULL;
	}
}

struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv {
	struct delayed_work connect;
	struct net_device *lowerdev;
	struct net_device *upperdev;
	u32 tx_packets;
	u32 tx_failed;
	u32 connect_requested_ssid_len;
	u8 connect_requested_ssid[IEEE80211_MAX_SSID_LEN];
	u8 connect_requested_bss[ETH_ALEN];
	bool is_up;
	bool is_connected;
	bool being_deleted;
};

/* Called with the rtnl lock held. */
static int virt_wifi_connect(struct wiphy *wiphy, struct net_device *netdev,
			     struct cfg80211_connect_params *sme)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(netdev);
	bool could_schedule;

	if (priv->being_deleted || !priv->is_up)
		return -EBUSY;

	if (!sme->ssid)
		return -EINVAL;

	priv->connect_requested_ssid_len = sme->ssid_len;
	memcpy(priv->connect_requested_ssid, sme->ssid, sme->ssid_len);

	could_schedule = schedule_delayed_work(&priv->connect, HZ * 2);
	if (!could_schedule)
		return -EBUSY;

	if (sme->bssid) {
		ether_addr_copy(priv->connect_requested_bss, sme->bssid);
	} else {
		virt_wifi_inform_bss(wiphy);
		eth_zero_addr(priv->connect_requested_bss);
	}

	wiphy_debug(wiphy, "connect\n");

	return 0;
}

/* Acquires and releases the rdev event lock. */
static void virt_wifi_connect_complete(struct work_struct *work)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv =
		container_of(work, struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv, connect.work);
	u8 *requested_bss = priv->connect_requested_bss;
	bool right_addr = ether_addr_equal(requested_bss, fake_router_bssid);
	bool right_ssid = priv->connect_requested_ssid_len == virt_wifi_ssid_len &&
			  !memcmp(priv->connect_requested_ssid, virt_wifi_ssid,
				  priv->connect_requested_ssid_len);
	u16 status = WLAN_STATUS_SUCCESS;

	if (is_zero_ether_addr(requested_bss))
		requested_bss = NULL;

	if (!priv->is_up || (requested_bss && !right_addr) || !right_ssid)
		status = WLAN_STATUS_UNSPECIFIED_FAILURE;
	else
		priv->is_connected = true;

	/* Schedules an event that acquires the rtnl lock. */
	cfg80211_connect_result(priv->upperdev,
				priv->is_connected ? fake_router_bssid : NULL,
				NULL, 0, NULL, 0,
				status, GFP_KERNEL);
	netif_carrier_on(priv->upperdev);
}

/* May acquire and release the rdev event lock. */
static void virt_wifi_cancel_connect(struct net_device *netdev)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(netdev);

	/* If there is work pending, clean up dangling callbacks. */
	if (cancel_delayed_work_sync(&priv->connect)) {
		/* Schedules an event that acquires the rtnl lock. */
		cfg80211_connect_result(priv->upperdev,
					priv->connect_requested_bss, NULL, 0,
					NULL, 0,
					WLAN_STATUS_UNSPECIFIED_FAILURE,
					GFP_KERNEL);
	}
}

/* Called with the rtnl lock held. Acquires the rdev event lock. */
static int virt_wifi_disconnect(struct wiphy *wiphy, struct net_device *netdev,
				u16 reason_code)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(netdev);

	if (priv->being_deleted)
		return -EBUSY;

	wiphy_debug(wiphy, "disconnect\n");
	virt_wifi_cancel_connect(netdev);

	cfg80211_disconnected(netdev, reason_code, NULL, 0, true, GFP_KERNEL);
	priv->is_connected = false;
	netif_carrier_off(netdev);

	return 0;
}

/* Called with the rtnl lock held. */
static int virt_wifi_get_station(struct wiphy *wiphy, struct net_device *dev,
				 const u8 *mac, struct station_info *sinfo)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(dev);

	wiphy_debug(wiphy, "get_station\n");

	if (!priv->is_connected || !ether_addr_equal(mac, fake_router_bssid))
		return -ENOENT;

	sinfo->filled = BIT_ULL(NL80211_STA_INFO_TX_PACKETS) |
		BIT_ULL(NL80211_STA_INFO_TX_FAILED) |
		BIT_ULL(NL80211_STA_INFO_SIGNAL) |
		BIT_ULL(NL80211_STA_INFO_TX_BITRATE);
	sinfo->tx_packets = priv->tx_packets;
	sinfo->tx_failed = priv->tx_failed;
	/* For CFG80211_SIGNAL_TYPE_MBM, value is expressed in _dBm_ */
	sinfo->signal = -50;
	sinfo->txrate = (struct rate_info) {
		.legacy = 10, /* units are 100kbit/s */
	};
	return 0;
}

/* Called with the rtnl lock held. */
static int virt_wifi_dump_station(struct wiphy *wiphy, struct net_device *dev,
				  int idx, u8 *mac, struct station_info *sinfo)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(dev);

	wiphy_debug(wiphy, "dump_station\n");

	if (idx != 0 || !priv->is_connected)
		return -ENOENT;

	ether_addr_copy(mac, fake_router_bssid);
	return virt_wifi_get_station(wiphy, dev, fake_router_bssid, sinfo);
}

static const struct cfg80211_ops virt_wifi_cfg80211_ops = {
	.scan = virt_wifi_scan,

	.connect = virt_wifi_connect,
	.disconnect = virt_wifi_disconnect,

	.get_station = virt_wifi_get_station,
	.dump_station = virt_wifi_dump_station,
};

/* Acquires and releases the rtnl lock. */
static struct wiphy *virt_wifi_make_wiphy(void)
{
	struct wiphy *wiphy;
	struct virt_wifi_wiphy_priv *priv;
	int err;

	wiphy = wiphy_new(&virt_wifi_cfg80211_ops, sizeof(*priv));

	if (!wiphy)
		return NULL;

	wiphy->max_scan_ssids = 4;
	wiphy->max_scan_ie_len = 1000;
	wiphy->signal_type = CFG80211_SIGNAL_TYPE_MBM;

	wiphy->bands[NL80211_BAND_2GHZ] = &band_2ghz;
	wiphy->bands[NL80211_BAND_5GHZ] = &band_5ghz;
	wiphy->bands[NL80211_BAND_60GHZ] = NULL;

	wiphy->interface_modes = BIT(NL80211_IFTYPE_STATION);

	priv = wiphy_priv(wiphy);
	priv->being_deleted = false;
	priv->scan_request = NULL;
	INIT_DELAYED_WORK(&priv->scan_result, virt_wifi_scan_result);

	err = wiphy_register(wiphy);
	if (err < 0) {
		wiphy_free(wiphy);
		return NULL;
	}

	return wiphy;
}

/* Acquires and releases the rtnl lock. */
static void virt_wifi_destroy_wiphy(struct wiphy *wiphy)
{
	struct virt_wifi_wiphy_priv *priv;

	WARN(!wiphy, "%s called with null wiphy", __func__);
	if (!wiphy)
		return;

	priv = wiphy_priv(wiphy);
	priv->being_deleted = true;
	virt_wifi_cancel_scan(wiphy);

	if (wiphy->registered)
		wiphy_unregister(wiphy);
	wiphy_free(wiphy);
}

/* Enters and exits a RCU-bh critical section. */
static netdev_tx_t virt_wifi_start_xmit(struct sk_buff *skb,
					struct net_device *dev)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(dev);

	priv->tx_packets++;
	if (!priv->is_connected) {
		priv->tx_failed++;
		return NET_XMIT_DROP;
	}

	skb->dev = priv->lowerdev;
	return dev_queue_xmit(skb);
}

/* Called with rtnl lock held. */
static int virt_wifi_net_device_open(struct net_device *dev)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(dev);

	priv->is_up = true;
	return 0;
}

/* Called with rtnl lock held. */
static int virt_wifi_net_device_stop(struct net_device *dev)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *n_priv = netdev_priv(dev);

	n_priv->is_up = false;

	if (!dev->ieee80211_ptr)
		return 0;

	virt_wifi_cancel_scan(dev->ieee80211_ptr->wiphy);
	virt_wifi_cancel_connect(dev);
	netif_carrier_off(dev);

	return 0;
}

static int virt_wifi_net_device_get_iflink(const struct net_device *dev)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(dev);

	return READ_ONCE(priv->lowerdev->ifindex);
}

static const struct net_device_ops virt_wifi_ops = {
	.ndo_start_xmit = virt_wifi_start_xmit,
	.ndo_open	= virt_wifi_net_device_open,
	.ndo_stop	= virt_wifi_net_device_stop,
	.ndo_get_iflink = virt_wifi_net_device_get_iflink,
};

/* Invoked as part of rtnl lock release. */
static void virt_wifi_net_device_destructor(struct net_device *dev)
{
	/* Delayed past dellink to allow nl80211 to react to the device being
	 * deleted.
	 */
	kfree(dev->ieee80211_ptr);
	dev->ieee80211_ptr = NULL;
}

/* No lock interaction. */
static void virt_wifi_setup(struct net_device *dev)
{
	ether_setup(dev);
	dev->netdev_ops = &virt_wifi_ops;
	dev->needs_free_netdev  = true;
}

/* Called in a RCU read critical section from netif_receive_skb */
static rx_handler_result_t virt_wifi_rx_handler(struct sk_buff **pskb)
{
	struct sk_buff *skb = *pskb;
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv =
		rcu_dereference(skb->dev->rx_handler_data);

	if (!priv->is_connected)
		return RX_HANDLER_PASS;

	/* GFP_ATOMIC because this is a packet interrupt handler. */
	skb = skb_share_check(skb, GFP_ATOMIC);
	if (!skb) {
		dev_err(&priv->upperdev->dev, "can't skb_share_check\n");
		return RX_HANDLER_CONSUMED;
	}

	*pskb = skb;
	skb->dev = priv->upperdev;
	skb->pkt_type = PACKET_HOST;
	return RX_HANDLER_ANOTHER;
}

/* Called with rtnl lock held. */
static int virt_wifi_newlink(struct net *src_net, struct net_device *dev,
			     struct nlattr *tb[], struct nlattr *data[],
			     struct netlink_ext_ack *extack)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(dev);
	int err;

	if (!tb[IFLA_LINK])
		return -EINVAL;

	netif_carrier_off(dev);

	priv->upperdev = dev;
	priv->lowerdev = __dev_get_by_index(src_net,
					    nla_get_u32(tb[IFLA_LINK]));

	if (!priv->lowerdev)
		return -ENODEV;
	if (!tb[IFLA_MTU])
		dev->mtu = priv->lowerdev->mtu;
	else if (dev->mtu > priv->lowerdev->mtu)
		return -EINVAL;

	err = netdev_rx_handler_register(priv->lowerdev, virt_wifi_rx_handler,
					 priv);
	if (err) {
		dev_err(&priv->lowerdev->dev,
			"can't netdev_rx_handler_register: %d\n", err);
		return err;
	}

	eth_hw_addr_inherit(dev, priv->lowerdev);
	netif_stacked_transfer_operstate(priv->lowerdev, dev);

	dev->ieee80211_ptr = kzalloc(sizeof(*dev->ieee80211_ptr), GFP_KERNEL);

	if (!dev->ieee80211_ptr) {
		err = -ENOMEM;
		goto remove_handler;
	}

	dev->ieee80211_ptr->iftype = NL80211_IFTYPE_STATION;
	dev->ieee80211_ptr->wiphy = common_wiphy;

	err = register_netdevice(dev);
	if (err) {
		dev_err(&priv->lowerdev->dev, "can't register_netdevice: %d\n",
			err);
		goto free_wireless_dev;
	}

	err = netdev_upper_dev_link(priv->lowerdev, dev, extack);
	if (err) {
		dev_err(&priv->lowerdev->dev, "can't netdev_upper_dev_link: %d\n",
			err);
		goto unregister_netdev;
	}

	dev->priv_destructor = virt_wifi_net_device_destructor;
	priv->being_deleted = false;
	priv->is_connected = false;
	priv->is_up = false;
	INIT_DELAYED_WORK(&priv->connect, virt_wifi_connect_complete);
	__module_get(THIS_MODULE);

	return 0;
unregister_netdev:
	unregister_netdevice(dev);
free_wireless_dev:
	kfree(dev->ieee80211_ptr);
	dev->ieee80211_ptr = NULL;
remove_handler:
	netdev_rx_handler_unregister(priv->lowerdev);

	return err;
}

/* Called with rtnl lock held. */
static void virt_wifi_dellink(struct net_device *dev,
			      struct list_head *head)
{
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv = netdev_priv(dev);

	if (dev->ieee80211_ptr)
		virt_wifi_cancel_scan(dev->ieee80211_ptr->wiphy);

	priv->being_deleted = true;
	virt_wifi_cancel_connect(dev);
	netif_carrier_off(dev);

	netdev_rx_handler_unregister(priv->lowerdev);
	netdev_upper_dev_unlink(priv->lowerdev, dev);

	unregister_netdevice_queue(dev, head);
	module_put(THIS_MODULE);

	/* Deleting the wiphy is handled in the module destructor. */
}

static struct rtnl_link_ops virt_wifi_link_ops = {
	.kind		= "virt_wifi",
	.setup		= virt_wifi_setup,
	.newlink	= virt_wifi_newlink,
	.dellink	= virt_wifi_dellink,
	.priv_size	= sizeof(struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv),
};

static bool netif_is_virt_wifi_dev(const struct net_device *dev)
{
	return rcu_access_pointer(dev->rx_handler) == virt_wifi_rx_handler;
}

static int virt_wifi_event(struct notifier_block *this, unsigned long event,
			   void *ptr)
{
	struct net_device *lower_dev = netdev_notifier_info_to_dev(ptr);
	struct virt_wifi_netdev_priv *priv;
	struct net_device *upper_dev;
	LIST_HEAD(list_kill);

	if (!netif_is_virt_wifi_dev(lower_dev))
		return NOTIFY_DONE;

	switch (event) {
	case NETDEV_UNREGISTER:
		priv = rtnl_dereference(lower_dev->rx_handler_data);
		if (!priv)
			return NOTIFY_DONE;

		upper_dev = priv->upperdev;

		upper_dev->rtnl_link_ops->dellink(upper_dev, &list_kill);
		unregister_netdevice_many(&list_kill);
		break;
	}

	return NOTIFY_DONE;
}

static struct notifier_block virt_wifi_notifier = {
	.notifier_call = virt_wifi_event,
};

/* Acquires and releases the rtnl lock. */
static int __init virt_wifi_init_module(void)
{
	int err;

	size_t len = ssid ? strlen(ssid) : 0;

	if (len < 1 || len > IEEE80211_MAX_SSID_LEN) {
		pr_warn("virt_wifi: ssid must be 1 to %d bytes, using VirtWifi\n", IEEE80211_MAX_SSID_LEN);
		ssid = "VirtWifi";
		len = strlen(ssid);
	}
	memcpy(virt_wifi_ssid, ssid, len);
	virt_wifi_ssid_len = len;

	if (!bssid || !*bssid || !mac_pton(bssid, fake_router_bssid) || !is_valid_ether_addr(fake_router_bssid)) {
		if (bssid && *bssid)
			pr_warn("virt_wifi: bad bssid \"%s\", using a random one\n", bssid);
		/* Guaranteed to be locally-administered and not multicast. */
		eth_random_addr(fake_router_bssid);
	}

	err = register_netdevice_notifier(&virt_wifi_notifier);
	if (err)
		return err;

	err = -ENOMEM;
	common_wiphy = virt_wifi_make_wiphy();
	if (!common_wiphy)
		goto notifier;

	err = rtnl_link_register(&virt_wifi_link_ops);
	if (err)
		goto destroy_wiphy;

	return 0;

destroy_wiphy:
	virt_wifi_destroy_wiphy(common_wiphy);
notifier:
	unregister_netdevice_notifier(&virt_wifi_notifier);
	return err;
}

/* Acquires and releases the rtnl lock. */
static void __exit virt_wifi_cleanup_module(void)
{
	/* Will delete any devices that depend on the wiphy. */
	rtnl_link_unregister(&virt_wifi_link_ops);
	virt_wifi_destroy_wiphy(common_wiphy);
	unregister_netdevice_notifier(&virt_wifi_notifier);
}

module_init(virt_wifi_init_module);
module_exit(virt_wifi_cleanup_module);

MODULE_LICENSE("GPL v2");
MODULE_AUTHOR("Cody Schuffelen <schuffelen@google.com>");
MODULE_DESCRIPTION("Driver for a wireless wrapper of ethernet devices");
MODULE_ALIAS_RTNL_LINK("virt_wifi");

```

`source/qemu/hw/misc/vaddress-echo.c`:

```c
/*
 * vaddress sub-device 0x9c08: echoes the to-host stream back to the guest.
 * Test vehicle for the sub-device plumbing. Not what the guest's
 * vaddress_sample benchmark wants: it expects every u32 back incremented by
 * one.
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
 */

#include "qemu/osdep.h"
#include "qemu/log.h"
#include "hw/misc/vaddress.h"
#include "hw/misc/vaddress-ring.h"

/*
 * ponytail: only echoes what fits in the from-host ring right now; the guest
 * reader does not ring the doorbell, so a writer that fills both rings before
 * reading stalls until its next write. Add a host thread if that matters.
 */
static void echo_notify(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    uint8_t buf[4096];
    bool moved = false;

    for (;;) {
        ssize_t room = vas_ring_room(sd->mem, sd->size);
        ssize_t n = room <= 0 ? room :
            vas_ring_read(sd->mem, sd->size, buf, MIN(room, sizeof(buf)));
        if (n < 0) {
            qemu_log_mask(LOG_GUEST_ERROR,
                          "vaddress: echo %u: corrupt ring header\n", sd->id);
            return;
        }
        if (n == 0) {
            break;
        }
        vas_ring_write(sd->mem, sd->size, buf, n);
        moved = true;
    }
    if (moved && vas_guest_waiting(sd->mem)) {
        vaddress_raise_pin(sd);
    }
}

const VAddressSubDeviceOps vaddress_echo_ops = {
    .type = 0x9c08,
    .notify = echo_notify,
};

```

`source/qemu/hw/misc/vaddress-gl.c`:

```c
/*
 * vaddress sub-device 0x6701: the guest's GL and Vulkan streams (upstream gfxstream protocol).
 *
 * The bytes are handed unchanged to libkiln_gfx (kiln/gfx), the bridge to the gfxstream host
 * renderer. The library is dlopen'ed from the vaddress "gfx-lib" property; without it, BINDs are
 * refused. Posted frames are shown on a QEMU graphic console.
 *
 * With the "renderd" property the renderer runs in kiln-renderd, a process of its own: the stream
 * blocks are shared memory that renderd maps and pumps itself, and only small notifications cross
 * the socket (kiln-renderd.h, docs/PLAN-RENDERD.md). Frames then reach the player directly.
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
 */

#include "qemu/osdep.h"
#include "qemu/bswap.h"
#include "qemu/log.h"
#include "qemu/main-loop.h"
#include "qemu/thread.h"
#include "qemu/timer.h"
#include "qapi/error.h"
#include "qemu/error-report.h"
#include "qemu/sockets.h"
#include "ui/console.h"
#include "ui/surface.h"
#include "hw/misc/vaddress.h"
#include "hw/misc/vaddress-ring.h"
#include "hw/misc/kiln-renderd.h"
#include <gmodule.h>
#include <mach/mach_vm.h>
#include <servers/bootstrap.h>

/* C ABI of libkiln_gfx; source of truth is kiln/gfx/kiln_gfx.h */
typedef struct kiln_conn kiln_conn;
typedef void (*kiln_post_fn)(void *opaque, int width, int height,
                            const uint8_t *pixels);
typedef void (*kiln_wake_fn)(void *opaque);
typedef void (*kiln_map_fn)(uint64_t gpa, void *hva, uint64_t size);
typedef void (*kiln_unmap_fn)(uint64_t gpa, uint64_t size);
typedef void (*kiln_sync_inc_fn)(uint32_t howmuch);

static struct {
    int (*init)(int width, int height, kiln_post_fn post, void *opaque);
    kiln_conn *(*conn_new)(kiln_wake_fn wake, void *opaque);
    int (*conn_write)(kiln_conn *c, const uint8_t *data, size_t len);
    size_t (*conn_pending)(kiln_conn *c);
    size_t (*conn_read)(kiln_conn *c, uint8_t *buf, size_t len);
    void (*conn_free)(kiln_conn *c);
    void (*set_vm_ops)(kiln_map_fn map, kiln_unmap_fn unmap);
    void (*sync_set_inc)(kiln_sync_inc_fn inc);
    void (*sync_set_present)(int present);
    void (*sync_trigger_wait)(uint64_t glsync, uint64_t thread);
} kiln;
static bool gl_ready;

/* Called from renderer threads: take the BQL for the memory-region changes. */
static void gl_vm_map(uint64_t gpa, void *hva, uint64_t size)
{
    bool had = bql_locked();

    if (!had) {
        bql_lock();
    }
    vaddress_host_map(gpa, hva, size, NULL, 0);
    if (!had) {
        bql_unlock();
    }
}

static void gl_vm_unmap(uint64_t gpa, uint64_t size)
{
    bool had = bql_locked();

    if (!had) {
        bql_lock();
    }
    vaddress_host_unmap(gpa, size);
    if (!had) {
        bql_unlock();
    }
}

#define GL_IO_CHUNK     (64 * 1024)

/* Latest posted frame, handed from a renderer thread to the main loop. */
static QemuConsole *gl_con;
static QemuMutex frame_lock;
static uint8_t *frame;
static int frame_w, frame_h;
static bool frame_new;
static QEMUBH *frame_bh;

static void gl_frame_bh(void *opaque)
{
    DisplaySurface *ds;

    qemu_mutex_lock(&frame_lock);
    if (!frame_new) {
        qemu_mutex_unlock(&frame_lock);
        return;
    }
    ds = qemu_console_surface(gl_con);
    if (!ds || surface_width(ds) != frame_w || surface_height(ds) != frame_h) {
        ds = qemu_create_displaysurface(frame_w, frame_h);
        qemu_console_set_surface(gl_con, ds);
    }
    for (int y = 0; y < frame_h; y++) {
        memcpy((uint8_t *)surface_data(ds) + y * surface_stride(ds),
               frame + (size_t)y * frame_w * 4, (size_t)frame_w * 4);
    }
    frame_new = false;
    qemu_mutex_unlock(&frame_lock);
    qemu_console_update_full(gl_con);
}

/* Renderer thread: copy and let the main loop draw it. */
static void gl_post(void *opaque, int width, int height, const uint8_t *pixels)
{
    size_t len = (size_t)width * height * 4;

    qemu_mutex_lock(&frame_lock);
    if (width != frame_w || height != frame_h) {
        frame = g_realloc(frame, len);
        frame_w = width;
        frame_h = height;
    }
    memcpy(frame, pixels, len);
    frame_new = true;
    qemu_mutex_unlock(&frame_lock);
    qemu_bh_schedule(frame_bh);
}

/*
 * Sub-device 0x5f01: the GPU fence timeline behind the guest's native sync. The guest driver (vaddress.ko) numbers its
 * fences in queue order; the doorbell hands one work item (the host sync object gfxstream made for it) to gfxstream's
 * SyncThread, which counts it as passed once the host GPU is past it. The count goes into the block and the pin wakes
 * the guest, which signals every fence up to it. One instance per VM.
 */
#define SYNC_GLSYNC     0x00
#define SYNC_THREAD     0x08
#define SYNC_COMPLETED  0x10

static VAddressSubDevice *sync_sd;  /* BQL */
static QEMUBH *sync_bh;
static uint64_t sync_completed;     /* atomic: SyncThread workers add, the BH publishes */
static uint64_t sync_base;          /* sync_completed at BIND: a rebooted guest numbers its fences from 1 again */

/* gfxstream's SyncThread workers: a host fence passed. */
static void sync_inc(uint32_t howmuch)
{
    qatomic_add(&sync_completed, howmuch);
    qemu_bh_schedule(sync_bh);
}

static void sync_bh_fn(void *opaque)
{
    if (sync_sd) {
        stq_le_p(sync_sd->mem + SYNC_COMPLETED, qatomic_read(&sync_completed) - sync_base);
        vaddress_raise_pin(sync_sd);
    }
}

static bool sync_bind(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    if (!gl_ready || sync_sd || sd->size < SYNC_COMPLETED + 8) {
        return false;
    }
    sync_sd = sd;
    sync_base = qatomic_read(&sync_completed);
    stq_le_p(sd->mem + SYNC_COMPLETED, 0);
    kiln.sync_set_present(1);
    return true;
}

static void sync_notify(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    kiln.sync_trigger_wait(ldq_le_p(sd->mem + SYNC_GLSYNC), ldq_le_p(sd->mem + SYNC_THREAD));
}

static void sync_unbind(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    kiln.sync_set_present(0);
    sync_sd = NULL;
}

const VAddressSubDeviceOps vaddress_sync_ops = {
    .type = VADDRESS_SUBDEV_SYNC,
    .bind = sync_bind,
    .notify = sync_notify,
    .unbind = sync_unbind,
};

static const GraphicHwOps gl_console_ops = {};

bool vaddress_gl_init(DeviceState *dev, const char *path, uint32_t width,
                      uint32_t height, Error **errp)
{
    static const char *const names[] = {
        "kiln_init", "kiln_conn_new", "kiln_conn_write",
        "kiln_conn_pending", "kiln_conn_read", "kiln_conn_free", "kiln_set_vm_ops",
        "kiln_sync_set_inc", "kiln_sync_set_present", "kiln_sync_trigger_wait",
    };
    gpointer *slots[] = {
        (gpointer *)&kiln.init, (gpointer *)&kiln.conn_new,
        (gpointer *)&kiln.conn_write, (gpointer *)&kiln.conn_pending,
        (gpointer *)&kiln.conn_read, (gpointer *)&kiln.conn_free,
        (gpointer *)&kiln.set_vm_ops,
        (gpointer *)&kiln.sync_set_inc, (gpointer *)&kiln.sync_set_present,
        (gpointer *)&kiln.sync_trigger_wait,
    };
    GModule *mod = g_module_open(path, G_MODULE_BIND_LOCAL);

    if (!mod) {
        error_setg(errp, "vaddress: cannot load gfx-lib '%s': %s", path,
                   g_module_error());
        return false;
    }
    for (size_t i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(names); i++) {
        if (!g_module_symbol(mod, names[i], slots[i])) {
            error_setg(errp, "vaddress: %s: missing symbol %s", path,
                       names[i]);
            g_module_close(mod);
            return false;
        }
    }
    g_module_make_resident(mod);

    kiln.set_vm_ops(gl_vm_map, gl_vm_unmap);
    sync_bh = qemu_bh_new(sync_bh_fn, NULL);
    kiln.sync_set_inc(sync_inc);
    qemu_mutex_init(&frame_lock);
    frame_bh = qemu_bh_new(gl_frame_bh, NULL);
    gl_con = qemu_graphic_console_create(dev, 0, &gl_console_ops, NULL);
    if (!kiln.init(width, height, gl_post, NULL)) {
        error_setg(errp, "vaddress: GL renderer failed to start (see stderr)");
        return false;
    }
    gl_ready = true;
    return true;
}

/*
 * One poller thread per stream: while the stream is active the thread watches
 * both rings itself and publishes host_state with bit 0 set, so the guest skips the doorbell (a VM
 * exit plus a hop through the main loop). When it has been idle for a moment it publishes a new even
 * host_state and sleeps; the next guest write then rings exactly once (vaddress-guest.md,
 * "Notification protocol"). The main loop is only used to raise the interrupt pin, and only when a
 * guest thread is actually asleep.
 */
#define GL_SPIN_NS      100000   /* keep polling this long after the last byte moved */
#define GL_SLEEP_MS     2        /* re-check even without a kick: belt and braces */

typedef struct GLStream {
    VAddressSubDevice *sd;
    kiln_conn *conn;
    QEMUBH *wake_bh;     /* raise the pin from the main loop */
    QemuThread thread;
    QemuMutex lock;
    QemuCond cond;
    QemuMutex ring_lock; /* held while the poller uses sd->mem/size; gl_rebind takes it to switch them */
    bool kick;           /* doorbell or host reply since the poller last looked */
    bool stop;
    bool broken;
    uint32_t sleep_gen;  /* idle host_state values: even, nonzero, never reused */
    uint8_t *buf;
} GLStream;

static void gl_wake_bh(void *opaque)
{
    GLStream *st = opaque;

    vaddress_raise_pin(st->sd);
}

static void gl_kick(GLStream *st)
{
    qemu_mutex_lock(&st->lock);
    st->kick = true;
    qemu_cond_signal(&st->cond);
    qemu_mutex_unlock(&st->lock);
}

/* Move bytes both ways; no BQL. Returns whether anything moved. */
static bool gl_pump_locked(GLStream *st);

static bool gl_pump(GLStream *st)
{
    bool moved;

    qemu_mutex_lock(&st->ring_lock);
    moved = gl_pump_locked(st);
    qemu_mutex_unlock(&st->ring_lock);
    return moved;
}

/* Publish host_state in the ring the instance has now. */
static void gl_store_state(GLStream *st, uint32_t v)
{
    qemu_mutex_lock(&st->ring_lock);
    vas_store(st->sd->mem, VAS_HOST_STATE, v);
    qemu_mutex_unlock(&st->ring_lock);
}

static bool gl_pump_locked(GLStream *st)
{
    VAddressSubDevice *sd = st->sd;
    bool in = false, out = false;
    ssize_t n;

    while ((n = vas_ring_read(sd->mem, sd->size, st->buf, GL_IO_CHUNK)) > 0) {
        in = true;
        if (!st->broken && !kiln.conn_write(st->conn, st->buf, n)) {
            st->broken = true;
            qemu_log_mask(LOG_GUEST_ERROR,
                          "vaddress: gl %u: stream broken, dropping\n", sd->id);
        }
    }
    if (n < 0) {
        qemu_log_mask(LOG_GUEST_ERROR,
                      "vaddress: gl %u: corrupt ring header\n", sd->id);
    }

    while (kiln.conn_pending(st->conn)) {
        ssize_t room = vas_ring_room(sd->mem, sd->size);

        if (room < 0) {
            qemu_log_mask(LOG_GUEST_ERROR,
                          "vaddress: gl %u: corrupt ring header\n", sd->id);
            break;
        }
        if (room == 0) {
            break; /* the guest reader drains it; we look again on the next pass */
        }
        n = kiln.conn_read(st->conn, st->buf, MIN(room, GL_IO_CHUNK));
        if (n == 0) {
            break;
        }
        vas_ring_write(sd->mem, sd->size, st->buf, n);
        out = true;
    }

    /* Publish the ring positions before looking at what the guest is doing. */
    smp_mb();
    if ((in || out) && vas_guest_waiting(sd->mem)) {
        qemu_bh_schedule(st->wake_bh);
    } else if (!out && vas_reader_blocked(sd->mem) && vas_ring_unread(sd->mem, sd->size)) {
        /* A reader still marked asleep with replies sitting unread: kick it again. */
        static unsigned kicks;

        qemu_bh_schedule(st->wake_bh);
        if (kicks++ < 20) {
            fprintf(stderr, "vaddress: gl %u: safety kick #%u (guest asleep with unread "
                    "replies)\n", sd->id, kicks);
        }
    }
    return in || out;
}

static void *gl_poller(void *opaque)
{
    GLStream *st = opaque;
    int64_t last_move = qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME);

    gl_store_state(st, 1); /* active: the guest need not ring */
    for (;;) {
        if (gl_pump(st)) {
            last_move = qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME);
            continue;
        }
        qemu_mutex_lock(&st->lock);
        if (st->stop) {
            qemu_mutex_unlock(&st->lock);
            break;
        }
        if (st->kick) {
            st->kick = false;
            qemu_mutex_unlock(&st->lock);
            continue;
        }
        qemu_mutex_unlock(&st->lock);

        if (qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) - last_move < GL_SPIN_NS) {
            cpu_relax();
            continue;
        }

        /* Idle: tell the guest to ring on its next write, then re-check for a race. */
        st->sleep_gen += 2;
        gl_store_state(st, st->sleep_gen);
        smp_mb();
        if (gl_pump(st)) {
            gl_store_state(st, 1);
            last_move = qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME);
            continue;
        }
        /*
         * Asleep until a doorbell or a host reply kicks us. The timed wake-up only re-checks the rings
         * (a lost doorbell); with nothing there the stream sleeps on without going active. Waking active
         * on every timeout made each idle stream spin GL_SPIN_NS 500 times a second: with Genshin's 28
         * streams that was 1.3 host cores doing nothing.
         */
        for (;;) {
            bool kicked, stop;

            qemu_mutex_lock(&st->lock);
            if (!st->kick && !st->stop) {
                qemu_cond_timedwait(&st->cond, &st->lock, GL_SLEEP_MS);
            }
            kicked = st->kick;
            stop = st->stop;
            st->kick = false;
            qemu_mutex_unlock(&st->lock);
            if (stop || kicked || gl_pump(st)) {
                break;
            }
        }
        gl_store_state(st, 1);
        last_move = qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME);
    }
    return NULL;
}

/*
 * kiln-renderd transport. Every send happens with the BQL held (sub-device callbacks, the main loop), so the socket
 * needs no lock of its own. Renderd's messages are read in the main loop.
 */
static bool rd_mode;
static int rd_sock = -1;
static GHashTable *rd_streams; /* sub-device id -> VAddressSubDevice; BQL */

/*
 * Renderd died or stopped reading. The VM keeps running without a GPU, so the guest can still be shut down cleanly
 * (the player offers it); its GPU streams just stop. ponytail: telling the guest needs a host-side close in the
 * vaddress driver, and is only worth it with a renderd that can be restarted.
 */
static void rd_lost(const char *why)
{
    error_report("vaddress: kiln-renderd %s: the guest has no GPU any more", why);
    qemu_set_fd_handler(rd_sock, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    close(rd_sock);
    rd_sock = -1;
}

static void rd_send(uint32_t type, uint32_t stream, uint64_t a, uint64_t b, int fd)
{
    KrdMsg m = { .type = type, .stream = stream, .a = a, .b = b };
    g_autofree char *why = NULL;

    if (rd_sock < 0) {
        return; /* renderd is gone (logged when it went) */
    }
    if (krd_send(rd_sock, &m, fd, 0) < 0) {
        why = g_strdup_printf("stopped taking messages (%s)", strerror(errno));
        rd_lost(why);
    }
}

static void rd_sync_set_present(int present)
{
    rd_send(KRD_SYNC_PRESENT, 0, present, 0, -1);
}

static void rd_sync_trigger_wait(uint64_t glsync, uint64_t thread)
{
    rd_send(KRD_SYNC_TRIGGER, 0, glsync, thread, -1);
}

static void rd_read(void *opaque)
{
    char peek;

    /*
     * Everything renderd has sent, not one message per main-loop pass: under load it sends thousands of RAISEs a
     * second, and a full socket makes it drop them. Bounded, so other handlers still run.
     */
    for (int i = 0; i < 256 && rd_sock >= 0; i++) {
        VAddressSubDevice *sd;
        KrdMsg m;
        int fd, r = krd_recv(rd_sock, &m, &fd);

        if (fd >= 0) {
            close(fd); /* no renderd message carries one in this version */
        }
        if (r <= 0) {
            rd_lost(r == 0 ? "closed the connection" : strerror(errno));
            return;
        }
        switch (m.type) {
        case KRD_RAISE:
            sd = g_hash_table_lookup(rd_streams, GUINT_TO_POINTER(m.stream));
            if (sd) { /* else unbound since renderd sent it */
                vaddress_raise_pin(sd);
            }
            break;
        case KRD_SYNC_INC:
            sync_inc(m.a);
            break;
        default:
            error_report_once("vaddress: kiln-renderd sent unknown message %u", m.type);
        }
        if (recv(rd_sock, &peek, 1, MSG_PEEK | MSG_DONTWAIT) <= 0) {
            break; /* nothing more now (or the end, which the next pass reads) */
        }
    }
}

/* Renderd's vm ops (kiln-renderd.h): map its pages into QEMU, then at the guest-physical address. */
static int32_t rd_vm_map(KrdVmMsg *m)
{
    mach_vm_address_t base = 0;
    kern_return_t kr;
    bool ok;

    if (m->body.msgh_descriptor_count != 1 || m->offset + m->size > m->entry_size) {
        error_report("vaddress: kiln-renderd sent a malformed map of gpa 0x%" PRIx64, m->gpa);
        return 0;
    }
    kr = mach_vm_map(mach_task_self(), &base, m->entry_size, 0, VM_FLAGS_ANYWHERE, m->entry.name, 0, false,
                     VM_PROT_READ | VM_PROT_WRITE, VM_PROT_READ | VM_PROT_WRITE, VM_INHERIT_NONE);
    if (kr != KERN_SUCCESS) {
        error_report("vaddress: mapping renderer memory for gpa 0x%" PRIx64 " (0x%" PRIx64 " bytes): %s",
                     m->gpa, m->entry_size, mach_error_string(kr));
        return 0;
    }
    bql_lock();
    ok = vaddress_host_map(m->gpa, (uint8_t *)base + m->offset, m->size, (void *)base, m->entry_size);
    bql_unlock();
    if (!ok) {
        mach_vm_deallocate(mach_task_self(), base, m->entry_size);
    }
    return ok;
}

static void *rd_vm_thread(void *opaque)
{
    mach_port_t port = (mach_port_t)(uintptr_t)opaque;

    for (;;) {
        struct {
            KrdVmMsg m;
            mach_msg_trailer_t trailer;
        } in;
        KrdVmReply r = { .ok = 0 };
        kern_return_t kr = mach_msg(&in.m.h, MACH_RCV_MSG, 0, sizeof(in), port, MACH_MSG_TIMEOUT_NONE,
                                    MACH_PORT_NULL);

        if (kr != KERN_SUCCESS) {
            error_report("vaddress: renderer vm ops port: %s", mach_error_string(kr));
            continue;
        }
        if (in.m.h.msgh_id == KRD_VM_MAP) {
            r.ok = rd_vm_map(&in.m);
        } else if (in.m.h.msgh_id == KRD_VM_UNMAP) {
            bql_lock();
            vaddress_host_unmap(in.m.gpa, in.m.size);
            bql_unlock();
            r.ok = 1;
        } else {
            error_report_once("vaddress: renderer vm ops: unknown message 0x%x", in.m.h.msgh_id);
        }
        if (in.m.body.msgh_descriptor_count) {
            mach_port_deallocate(mach_task_self(), in.m.entry.name); /* mapped (or refused): the right is done */
        }
        r.h.msgh_bits = MACH_MSGH_BITS(MACH_MSGH_BITS_REMOTE(in.m.h.msgh_bits), 0);
        r.h.msgh_size = sizeof(r);
        r.h.msgh_remote_port = in.m.h.msgh_remote_port;
        r.h.msgh_id = in.m.h.msgh_id;
        kr = mach_msg(&r.h, MACH_SEND_MSG, sizeof(r), 0, MACH_PORT_NULL, MACH_MSG_TIMEOUT_NONE, MACH_PORT_NULL);
        if (kr != KERN_SUCCESS) {
            error_report_once("vaddress: renderer vm ops reply: %s", mach_error_string(kr));
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

/* Checks in the vm ops port before HELLO, so renderd finds it when it starts the renderer. */
static bool rd_vm_start(Error **errp)
{
    g_autofree char *name = g_strdup_printf(KRD_VM_SERVICE "%d", (int)getpid());
    static QemuThread thread;
    mach_port_t port;
    kern_return_t kr = bootstrap_check_in(bootstrap_port, name, &port);

    if (kr != KERN_SUCCESS) {
        error_setg(errp, "vaddress: cannot register %s for kiln-renderd: %s", name, bootstrap_strerror(kr));
        return false;
    }
    qemu_thread_create(&thread, "kiln-vm-ops", rd_vm_thread, (void *)(uintptr_t)port, QEMU_THREAD_DETACHED);
    return true;
}

bool vaddress_renderd_init(DeviceState *dev, const char *path, uint32_t width,
                           uint32_t height, Error **errp)
{
    KrdMsg m = { .type = KRD_HELLO, .a = KRD_VERSION, .b = (uint64_t)width << 32 | height };
    int fd, one = 1, sndbuf = 1024 * 1024;
    struct timeval timeout = { .tv_sec = 10 };

    if (!rd_vm_start(errp)) {
        return false;
    }
    rd_sock = unix_connect(path, errp);
    if (rd_sock < 0) {
        error_prepend(errp, "vaddress: kiln-renderd: ");
        return false;
    }
    setsockopt(rd_sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_NOSIGPIPE, &one, sizeof(one));
    /*
     * Sends happen with the BQL held: a renderd that stops reading must not freeze the VM. The buffer rides out renderd
     * being busy (an UNBIND waits for the renderer's current command, a shader compile can take seconds); past the
     * timeout renderd counts as lost.
     */
    setsockopt(rd_sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_SNDBUF, &sndbuf, sizeof(sndbuf));
    setsockopt(rd_sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_SNDTIMEO, &timeout, sizeof(timeout));
    /* Renderd starts the renderer before it answers, as kiln_init does in process. */
    if (krd_send(rd_sock, &m, -1, 0) < 0 || krd_recv(rd_sock, &m, &fd) <= 0) {
        error_setg_errno(errp, errno, "vaddress: kiln-renderd at %s did not answer", path);
        return false;
    }
    if (m.type != KRD_HELLO || m.a != KRD_VERSION || !m.b) {
        error_setg(errp, "vaddress: kiln-renderd %s", m.type == KRD_HELLO && m.a == KRD_VERSION
                   ? "could not start the renderer (see its log)" : "speaks another protocol version");
        return false;
    }
    rd_mode = true;
    rd_streams = g_hash_table_new(NULL, NULL);
    kiln.sync_set_present = rd_sync_set_present;
    kiln.sync_trigger_wait = rd_sync_trigger_wait;
    sync_bh = qemu_bh_new(sync_bh_fn, NULL);
    /* usb-kbd/usb-tablet name this device's console (display=va0); it never shows a frame in this mode. */
    gl_con = qemu_graphic_console_create(dev, 0, &gl_console_ops, NULL);
    qemu_set_fd_handler(rd_sock, rd_read, NULL, NULL);
    gl_ready = true;
    return true;
}

static bool rd_bind(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    if (rd_sock < 0 || sd->fd < 0) {
        return false;
    }
    g_hash_table_insert(rd_streams, GUINT_TO_POINTER(sd->id), sd);
    rd_send(KRD_BIND, sd->id, sd->size, 0, sd->fd);
    return true;
}

/*
 * Renderd moves over when it reads this. The guest only grows a stream whose rings are empty while the host side
 * sleeps (vaddress-guest.md, "Ring growth"), and its next write rings the doorbell, whose KICK comes after this.
 */
static void rd_rebind(VAddressSubDevice *sd, uint8_t *mem, uint64_t size)
{
    sd->mem = mem;
    sd->size = size;
    rd_send(KRD_REBIND, sd->id, size, 0, sd->fd);
}

static void rd_unbind(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    /* Renderd keeps its own mapping until it reads this, so QEMU may free the block at once. */
    g_hash_table_remove(rd_streams, GUINT_TO_POINTER(sd->id));
    rd_send(KRD_UNBIND, sd->id, 0, 0, -1);
}

/* libkiln_gfx reader thread: a reply is ready */
static void gl_wake(void *opaque)
{
    gl_kick(opaque);
}

static bool gfxstream_bind(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    GLStream *st;

    if (!gl_ready) {
        return false;
    }
    if (rd_mode) {
        return rd_bind(sd);
    }
    st = g_new0(GLStream, 1);
    st->sd = sd;
    st->buf = g_malloc(GL_IO_CHUNK);
    st->wake_bh = qemu_bh_new(gl_wake_bh, st);
    qemu_mutex_init(&st->lock);
    qemu_mutex_init(&st->ring_lock);
    qemu_cond_init(&st->cond);
    st->conn = kiln.conn_new(gl_wake, st);
    sd->opaque = st;
    if (getenv("KILN_NAMES")) {
        /* KILN_NAMES=1: who opens a stream (the guest writes its cmdline into the header). */
        char name[VAS_NAME_SIZE + 1] = {};
        static unsigned seq;

        memcpy(name, sd->mem + VAS_NAME, VAS_NAME_SIZE);
        fprintf(stderr, "vaddress: gl %u bind #%u '%s'\n", sd->id, ++seq, name);
    }
    qemu_thread_create(&st->thread, "kiln-poll", gl_poller, st, QEMU_THREAD_JOINABLE);
    return true;
}

static void gl_rebind(VAddressSubDevice *sd, uint8_t *mem, uint64_t size)
{
    GLStream *st = sd->opaque;

    if (rd_mode) {
        rd_rebind(sd, mem, size);
        return;
    }
    qemu_mutex_lock(&st->ring_lock);
    sd->mem = mem;
    sd->size = size;
    qemu_mutex_unlock(&st->ring_lock);
    gl_kick(st);
}

static void gl_notify(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    if (rd_mode) {
        rd_send(KRD_KICK, sd->id, 0, 0, -1);
        return;
    }
    gl_kick(sd->opaque);
}

static void gl_unbind(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    GLStream *st = sd->opaque;

    if (rd_mode) {
        rd_unbind(sd);
        return;
    }
    /* The poller must stop touching the ring (and the connection) before either goes away. */
    qemu_mutex_lock(&st->lock);
    st->stop = true;
    qemu_cond_signal(&st->cond);
    qemu_mutex_unlock(&st->lock);
    qemu_thread_join(&st->thread);
    kiln.conn_free(st->conn); /* joins its threads: no more gl_wake */
    qemu_bh_delete(st->wake_bh);
    qemu_cond_destroy(&st->cond);
    qemu_mutex_destroy(&st->lock);
    qemu_mutex_destroy(&st->ring_lock);
    g_free(st->buf);
    g_free(st);
}

const VAddressSubDeviceOps vaddress_gfxstream_ops = {
    .type = VADDRESS_SUBDEV_GFXSTREAM,
    .bind = gfxstream_bind,
    .rebind = gl_rebind,
    .notify = gl_notify,
    .unbind = gl_unbind,
};

```

`source/qemu/hw/misc/vaddress.c`:

```c
/*
 * vaddress: address-space PCI device.
 *
 * Written from the behavioral specs in kiln/docs/vaddress.md and
 * kiln/docs/vaddress-guest.md; no vendor code is used.
 *
 * Implemented: identity, the three BARs, the BAR0 register file, block
 * ALLOCATE/DEALLOCATE, MAP_HVA/UNMAP_HVA with host-allocated memory (hint 0),
 * SET_PARAM(MEM_BAR_GPA), sub-devices (BIND/TERMINATE, doorbell, interrupt
 * pins through the BAR1 ring). BIND of a sub-device type with no host
 * implementation is refused, so guest clients fail cleanly instead of
 * blocking on a ring nobody serves.
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
 */

#include "qemu/osdep.h"
#include "qemu/log.h"
#include "qemu/units.h"
#include "qapi/error.h"
#include "qemu/error-report.h"
#include "hw/pci/pci_device.h"
#include "hw/pci/msi.h"
#include "hw/core/qdev-properties.h"
#include "migration/vmstate.h"
#include "hw/misc/vaddress.h"
#include "qom/object.h"

#define TYPE_VADDRESS "vaddress"
OBJECT_DECLARE_SIMPLE_TYPE(VAddressState, VADDRESS)

#define TYPE_VADDRESS_HOSTMEM "vaddress-hostmem"
OBJECT_DECLARE_SIMPLE_TYPE(VAddressHostMem, VADDRESS_HOSTMEM)

#define VADDRESS_VENDOR_ID      0x1c87
#define VADDRESS_DEVICE_ID      0x1074
#define VADDRESS_CLASS_ID       0x0500 /* PCI_CLASS_MEMORY_RAM */

#define VADDRESS_REGS_SIZE      0x40
#define VADDRESS_CONTROL_SIZE   (1 * MiB)
#define VADDRESS_MEM_SIZE       (8 * GiB)

/* BAR0 register offsets */
#define REG_COMMAND             0x00
#define REG_STATUS              0x04
#define REG_ARG0                0x08
#define REG_ARG1                0x10
#define REG_ARG2                0x18
#define REG_ARG3                0x20
#define REG_ARG4                0x28
#define REG_ISR                 0x2c
#define REG_PING_SUB_DEVICE_ID  0x30

/* BAR1: ring of pending interrupt pins (host produces, guest consumes) */
#define CTRL_PIN_BITS           0x00
#define CTRL_PIN_W              0x20
#define CTRL_PIN_R              0x24
#define CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN       32
#define CTRL_PIN_BROADCAST      0xff /* any value >= 32 wakes every waiter */

enum {
    CMD_ALLOCATE_BLOCK = 0,
    CMD_DEALLOCATE_BLOCK = 1,
    CMD_MAP_HVA = 2,
    CMD_UNMAP_HVA = 3,
    CMD_BIND_SUB_DEVICE = 5,
    CMD_TERMINATE_SUB_DEVICE = 8,
    CMD_SET_PARAM = 9,
};

enum {
    PARAM_MEM_BAR_GPA = 0,
    PARAM_IRQ_MODE = 1,
};

/*
 * STATUS values are guest ABI, so use fixed Linux errno numbers rather than
 * the host's <errno.h> (macOS numbers differ). The guest only tests != 0.
 */
#define VA_OK           0
#define VA_ENOMEM       12
#define VA_EEXIST       17
#define VA_ENODEV       19
#define VA_EINVAL       22
#define VA_EOPNOTSUPP   95

/* MAP_HVA hint values with a special meaning. */
#define HVA_HINT_HOST_ALLOC 0
#define HVA_HINT_REMOTE     1

/*
 * MAP_HVA results are opaque to the guest (it only echoes them back), so hand
 * out a cookie derived from the block offset instead of leaking host pointers.
 */
#define HVA_COOKIE_BASE (1ULL << 47)

/*
 * Owner of one host-memory mapping. A separate QOM object so the RAM region
 * can be torn down while the device lives: flatviews hold references to this
 * object, which frees itself (and the RAM, via RCU) when the last one drops.
 */
struct VAddressHostMem {
    Object parent_obj;
    MemoryRegion mr;
    void *own;      /* renderd mode: a mapping behind mr this object unmaps (shm block or renderer pages), else NULL */
    uint64_t own_size;
```
