```c
    int fd;         /* the shm block's fd when own is one, else -1 */
};

typedef struct VAddressBlock {
    uint64_t offset;
    uint64_t size;
    VAddressHostMem *hostmem; /* NULL when not mapped */
    uint64_t map_size;
    bool host_mapped;         /* hostmem is renderer memory (vaddress_host_map), not guest-allocated RAM */
    VAddressSubDevice *sd;    /* NULL when not bound */
} VAddressBlock;

struct VAddressState {
    PCIDevice parent_obj;

    MemoryRegion regs_mr;
    MemoryRegion control_mr;
    MemoryRegion mem_mr;

    uint32_t regs[VADDRESS_REGS_SIZE / 4];
    uint64_t mem_bar_gpa;
    /* ponytail: sorted list with O(n) first-fit; use an interval tree if
     * block counts reach the thousands (heavy Vulkan use). */
    GList *blocks;
    uint32_t next_sd_id;
    uint32_t next_pin;

    /* GL backend (sub-device 0x6701) */
    char *renderd;  /* kiln-renderd's socket: the renderer runs there and stream blocks are shared memory */
    char *gfx_lib;
    uint32_t gfx_width;
    uint32_t gfx_height;
};

static const VAddressSubDeviceOps *const va_sd_types[] = {
    &vaddress_sync_ops,
    &vaddress_echo_ops,
    &vaddress_gfxstream_ops,
};

static uint64_t va_get_u64(VAddressState *s, hwaddr off)
{
    return deposit64(s->regs[off / 4], 32, 32, s->regs[off / 4 + 1]);
}

static void va_set_u64(VAddressState *s, hwaddr off, uint64_t val)
{
    s->regs[off / 4] = val;
    s->regs[off / 4 + 1] = val >> 32;
}

static VAddressBlock *va_find_block(VAddressState *s, uint64_t offset)
{
    for (GList *l = s->blocks; l; l = l->next) {
        VAddressBlock *b = l->data;
        if (b->offset == offset) {
            return b;
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

static uint32_t va_allocate(VAddressState *s)
{
    uint64_t page = qemu_real_host_page_size();
    uint64_t req = va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG0);
    uint64_t size, start = 0;
    GList *l;

    if (req == 0 || req > VADDRESS_MEM_SIZE) {
        return VA_EINVAL;
    }
    size = ROUND_UP(req, page);

    /* First fit in the gaps between offset-sorted blocks. */
    for (l = s->blocks; l; l = l->next) {
        VAddressBlock *b = l->data;
        if (b->offset - start >= size) {
            break;
        }
        start = b->offset + b->size;
    }
    if (start + size > VADDRESS_MEM_SIZE) {
        return VA_ENOMEM;
    }

    VAddressBlock *nb = g_new0(VAddressBlock, 1);
    nb->offset = start;
    nb->size = size;
    s->blocks = g_list_insert_before(s->blocks, l, nb);

    va_set_u64(s, REG_ARG0, size);
    va_set_u64(s, REG_ARG1, start);
    return VA_OK;
}

static void va_terminate(VAddressBlock *b)
{
    VAddressSubDevice *sd = b->sd;

    if (!sd) {
        return;
    }
    if (sd->ops->unbind) {
        sd->ops->unbind(sd);
    }
    g_free(sd);
    b->sd = NULL;
}

static void va_unmap_block(VAddressState *s, VAddressBlock *b)
{
    if (!b->hostmem) {
        return;
    }
    /* The sub-device must stop touching the memory before it goes away. */
    va_terminate(b);
    memory_region_del_subregion(&s->mem_mr, &b->hostmem->mr);
    object_unparent(OBJECT(b->hostmem));
    b->hostmem = NULL;
    b->map_size = 0;
    b->host_mapped = false;
}

static void va_free_block(VAddressState *s, VAddressBlock *b)
{
    va_unmap_block(s, b);
    s->blocks = g_list_remove(s->blocks, b);
    g_free(b);
}

static uint32_t va_deallocate(VAddressState *s)
{
    VAddressBlock *b = va_find_block(s, va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG0));

    if (!b) {
        return VA_EINVAL;
    }
    va_free_block(s, b);
    return VA_OK;
}

/*
 * Renderd mode: a block's RAM is a POSIX shared memory object, so kiln-renderd can map the same rings. Unlinked at
 * once: only the fds keep it, QEMU's and the one renderd gets.
 */
static bool va_shm_init(VAddressHostMem *hm, uint64_t size)
{
    static unsigned seq;
    char name[32];
    void *p;
    int fd;

    snprintf(name, sizeof(name), "/kiln-va.%d.%u", (int)getpid(), seq++);
    fd = shm_open(name, O_RDWR | O_CREAT | O_EXCL, 0600);
    if (fd < 0) {
        error_report("vaddress: shm_open %s: %s", name, strerror(errno));
        return false;
    }
    shm_unlink(name);
    if (ftruncate(fd, size) < 0) {
        error_report("vaddress: ftruncate of shared block to 0x%" PRIx64 ": %s", size, strerror(errno));
        close(fd);
        return false;
    }
    p = mmap(NULL, size, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_SHARED, fd, 0);
    if (p == MAP_FAILED) {
        error_report("vaddress: mmap of shared block (0x%" PRIx64 " bytes): %s", size, strerror(errno));
        close(fd);
        return false;
    }
    memory_region_init_ram_ptr(&hm->mr, OBJECT(hm), "ram", size, p);
    hm->own = p;
    hm->own_size = size;
    hm->fd = fd;
    return true;
}

/* Flatviews held the last references: no vCPU or sub-device uses the memory any more. */
static void va_hostmem_finalize(Object *obj)
{
    VAddressHostMem *hm = VADDRESS_HOSTMEM(obj);

    if (hm->own) {
        munmap(hm->own, hm->own_size);
        if (hm->fd >= 0) {
            close(hm->fd);
        }
    }
}

static uint32_t va_map_hva(VAddressState *s)
{
    VAddressBlock *b = va_find_block(s, va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG0));
    uint64_t hint = va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG1);
    uint64_t size = ROUND_UP(va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG2),
                             qemu_real_host_page_size());
    g_autofree char *name = NULL;
    Error *err = NULL;
    VAddressHostMem *hm;

    if (!b || size == 0 || size > b->size) {
        return VA_EINVAL;
    }
    if (b->hostmem) {
        return VA_EEXIST;
    }
    if (hint != HVA_HINT_HOST_ALLOC) {
        /*
         * hint 1 is "remote memory", any other value is an existing host VA
         * (Vulkan host-visible memory). The guest must never pick host
         * addresses itself; both need a backend that vouches for the memory.
         */
        qemu_log_mask(LOG_UNIMP, "vaddress: MAP_HVA hint 0x%" PRIx64
                      " not supported\n", hint);
        return VA_EOPNOTSUPP;
    }

    hm = VADDRESS_HOSTMEM(object_new(TYPE_VADDRESS_HOSTMEM));
    name = g_strdup_printf("hostmem[0x%" PRIx64 "]", b->offset);
    if (s->renderd) {
        if (!va_shm_init(hm, size)) {
            object_unref(OBJECT(hm));
            return VA_ENOMEM;
        }
    } else if (!memory_region_init_ram_flags_nomigrate(&hm->mr, OBJECT(hm), "ram",
                                                       size, 0, &err)) {
        error_reportf_err(err, "vaddress: MAP_HVA of 0x%" PRIx64
                          " bytes at 0x%" PRIx64 " failed: ",
                          size, b->offset);
        object_unref(OBJECT(hm));
        return VA_ENOMEM;
    }
    object_property_add_child(OBJECT(s), name, OBJECT(hm));
    object_unref(OBJECT(hm)); /* the child property holds it now */
    memory_region_add_subregion(&s->mem_mr, b->offset, &hm->mr);

    b->hostmem = hm;
    b->map_size = size;
    va_set_u64(s, REG_ARG1, HVA_COOKIE_BASE + b->offset);
    va_set_u64(s, REG_ARG2, size);
    return VA_OK;
}

static VAddressState *va_active; /* the (single) device instance, for host-initiated maps */

/*
 * gfxstream's vkMapMemoryIntoAddressSpace: the host maps a Vulkan host-visible allocation at
 * the guest-physical address of a block the guest ALLOCATEd (and mmapped) earlier. The caller
 * holds the BQL.
 */
bool vaddress_host_map(uint64_t gpa, void *hva, uint64_t size, void *own, uint64_t own_size)
{
    VAddressState *s = va_active;
    VAddressBlock *b;
    VAddressHostMem *hm;
    g_autofree char *name = NULL;

    if (!s || gpa < s->mem_bar_gpa) {
        return false;
    }
    b = va_find_block(s, gpa - s->mem_bar_gpa);
    if (!b || b->hostmem || size == 0 || size > b->size) {
        qemu_log_mask(LOG_GUEST_ERROR, "vaddress: host map of 0x%" PRIx64 " bytes at gpa 0x%"
                      PRIx64 ": no free block there\n", size, gpa);
        return false;
    }
    hm = VADDRESS_HOSTMEM(object_new(TYPE_VADDRESS_HOSTMEM));
    name = g_strdup_printf("vkmem[0x%" PRIx64 "]", b->offset);
    memory_region_init_ram_ptr(&hm->mr, OBJECT(hm), "ram", size, hva);
    hm->own = own;
    hm->own_size = own_size;
    hm->fd = -1;
    object_property_add_child(OBJECT(s), name, OBJECT(hm));
    object_unref(OBJECT(hm)); /* the child property holds it now */
    memory_region_add_subregion(&s->mem_mr, b->offset, &hm->mr);
    b->hostmem = hm;
    b->map_size = size;
    b->host_mapped = true;
    return true;
}

void vaddress_host_unmap(uint64_t gpa, uint64_t size)
{
    VAddressState *s = va_active;
    VAddressBlock *b;

    if (!s || gpa < s->mem_bar_gpa) {
        return;
    }
    b = va_find_block(s, gpa - s->mem_bar_gpa);
    /*
     * The renderer unmaps by address only. If the guest already freed its block and the offset was reused, this must not
     * tear down the new owner (a live GL stream, say): only renderer memory of the size it mapped is ours to remove.
     */
    if (b && b->hostmem && b->host_mapped && b->map_size == size) {
        va_unmap_block(s, b);
    }
}

static uint32_t va_unmap_hva(VAddressState *s)
{
    VAddressBlock *b = va_find_block(s, va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG0));

    if (!b || !b->hostmem) {
        return VA_EINVAL;
    }
    va_unmap_block(s, b);
    return VA_OK;
}

static const VAddressSubDeviceOps *va_sd_ops(uint32_t type)
{
    for (size_t i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(va_sd_types); i++) {
        if (va_sd_types[i]->type == type) {
            return va_sd_types[i];
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

/*
 * BIND: G08 new block, G32 old block (== new when there is none), G40 type.
 * With an old block the existing instance moves to the new (bigger) block;
 * the guest never TERMINATEs the old one.
 */
static uint32_t va_bind(VAddressState *s)
{
    VAddressBlock *nb = va_find_block(s, va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG0));
    uint64_t old_offset = va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG3);
    uint32_t type = s->regs[REG_ARG4 / 4];
    VAddressSubDevice *sd;
    uint8_t *mem;

    if (!nb || !nb->hostmem || nb->sd) {
        return VA_EINVAL;
    }
    if (old_offset != nb->offset) {
        VAddressBlock *ob = va_find_block(s, old_offset);
        if (!ob || !ob->sd || ob->sd->ops->type != type) {
            return VA_EINVAL;
        }
        sd = ob->sd;
        ob->sd = NULL;
    } else {
        const VAddressSubDeviceOps *ops = va_sd_ops(type);
        if (!ops) {
            qemu_log_mask(LOG_UNIMP, "vaddress: no host side for sub-device"
                          " 0x%x\n", type);
            return VA_ENODEV;
        }
        sd = g_new0(VAddressSubDevice, 1);
        sd->dev = s;
        sd->ops = ops;
        sd->id = ++s->next_sd_id ?: ++s->next_sd_id; /* 0 means unbound */
        /* Spread instances over pins; a shared pin only costs spurious
         * wakeups. */
        sd->pin = s->next_pin++ % CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN;
    }
    mem = memory_region_get_ram_ptr(&nb->hostmem->mr);
    sd->fd = nb->hostmem->own ? nb->hostmem->fd : -1;
    if (old_offset != nb->offset && sd->ops->rebind) {
        sd->ops->rebind(sd, mem, nb->map_size);
    } else {
        sd->mem = mem;
        sd->size = nb->map_size;
    }
    if (old_offset == nb->offset && sd->ops->bind && !sd->ops->bind(sd)) {
        g_free(sd);
        return VA_ENODEV;
    }
    nb->sd = sd;
    s->regs[REG_ARG0 / 4] = sd->id;
    s->regs[REG_ARG1 / 4] = 1u << sd->pin;
    return VA_OK;
}

static uint32_t va_terminate_cmd(VAddressState *s)
{
    VAddressBlock *b = va_find_block(s, va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG0));

    if (!b) {
        return VA_EINVAL;
    }
    va_terminate(b);
    return VA_OK;
}

static void va_doorbell(VAddressState *s, uint32_t id)
{
    for (GList *l = s->blocks; l; l = l->next) {
        VAddressBlock *b = l->data;
        if (b->sd && b->sd->id == id) {
            b->sd->ops->notify(b->sd);
            return;
        }
    }
    qemu_log_mask(LOG_GUEST_ERROR, "vaddress: doorbell for unbound"
                  " sub-device %u\n", id);
}

void vaddress_raise_pin(VAddressSubDevice *sd)
{
    VAddressState *s = sd->dev;
    PCIDevice *pdev = PCI_DEVICE(s);
    uint8_t *ctrl = memory_region_get_ram_ptr(&s->control_mr);
    uint32_t *wp = (uint32_t *)(ctrl + CTRL_PIN_W);
    /* BAR1 is guest RAM: mask whatever is there. */
    uint32_t w = le32_to_cpu(qatomic_read(wp)) % CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN;
    uint32_t r = le32_to_cpu(qatomic_read((uint32_t *)(ctrl + CTRL_PIN_R))) %
                 CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN;
    bool pending = false;

    /*
     * No de-duplication against entries already in the ring: the guest may have
     * handled such an entry without advancing the read index yet, and skipping
     * the push would then leave a freshly sleeping waiter with no wakeup.
     */
    if (!pending) {
        uint32_t nw = (w + 1) % CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN;
        if (nw == r) {
            /* ponytail: ring full (31 other pins pending); turn the newest
             * entry into a broadcast. Lost if the guest is consuming that very
             * entry right now; a per-pin pending bitmap would close it. */
            ctrl[CTRL_PIN_BITS + (w + CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN - 1) %
                 CTRL_PIN_RING_LEN] = CTRL_PIN_BROADCAST;
        } else {
            ctrl[CTRL_PIN_BITS + w] = sd->pin;
            smp_wmb();
            qatomic_set(wp, cpu_to_le32(nw));
        }
    }
    if (msi_enabled(pdev)) {
        msi_notify(pdev, 0);
    } else {
        /* No ack register in ring mode: a level IRQ would storm. */
        pci_set_irq(pdev, 1);
        pci_set_irq(pdev, 0);
    }
}

static uint32_t va_set_param(VAddressState *s)
{
    uint32_t param = s->regs[REG_ARG0 / 4];
    /* value is u64 at 0x0c (lo) / 0x10 (hi), straddling ARG0/ARG1 */
    uint64_t value = va_get_u64(s, REG_ARG0 + 4);

    switch (param) {
    case PARAM_MEM_BAR_GPA:
        s->mem_bar_gpa = value;
        return VA_OK;
    case PARAM_IRQ_MODE:
        /* Only ring mode; refusing keeps the guest on it (as the original). */
        return VA_EOPNOTSUPP;
    default:
        return VA_EINVAL;
    }
}

static uint32_t va_run_command(VAddressState *s, uint32_t cmd)
{
    switch (cmd) {
    case CMD_ALLOCATE_BLOCK:
        return va_allocate(s);
    case CMD_DEALLOCATE_BLOCK:
        return va_deallocate(s);
    case CMD_MAP_HVA:
        return va_map_hva(s);
    case CMD_UNMAP_HVA:
        return va_unmap_hva(s);
    case CMD_BIND_SUB_DEVICE:
        return va_bind(s);
    case CMD_TERMINATE_SUB_DEVICE:
        return va_terminate_cmd(s);
    case CMD_SET_PARAM:
        return va_set_param(s);
    default:
        qemu_log_mask(LOG_GUEST_ERROR, "vaddress: invalid command %u\n", cmd);
        return VA_EINVAL;
    }
}

static uint64_t va_regs_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
{
    VAddressState *s = opaque;

    if (size != 4 || (addr & 3)) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (addr == REG_ISR) {
        return 0; /* PMIO irq mode is never enabled */
    }
    return s->regs[addr / 4];
}

static void va_regs_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t val,
                          unsigned size)
{
    VAddressState *s = opaque;

    if (size != 4 || (addr & 3)) {
        return;
    }
    s->regs[addr / 4] = val;
    switch (addr) {
    case REG_COMMAND:
        s->regs[REG_STATUS / 4] = va_run_command(s, val);
        break;
    case REG_PING_SUB_DEVICE_ID:
        va_doorbell(s, val);
        break;
    }
}

static const MemoryRegionOps va_regs_ops = {
    .read = va_regs_read,
    .write = va_regs_write,
    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
    /* Other access sizes are accepted and ignored / read 0. */
    .valid = { .min_access_size = 1, .max_access_size = 8 },
    .impl = { .min_access_size = 1, .max_access_size = 8 },
};

static void va_free_all_blocks(VAddressState *s)
{
    while (s->blocks) {
        va_free_block(s, s->blocks->data);
    }
}

static void va_reset(DeviceState *dev)
{
    VAddressState *s = VADDRESS(dev);

    va_free_all_blocks(s);
    memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
    s->mem_bar_gpa = 0;
    s->next_sd_id = 0;
    s->next_pin = 0;
    memset(memory_region_get_ram_ptr(&s->control_mr), 0,
           VADDRESS_CONTROL_SIZE);
}

static void va_realize(PCIDevice *pdev, Error **errp)
{
    VAddressState *s = VADDRESS(pdev);
    Error *err = NULL;
    int ret;

    va_active = s;
    pci_config_set_interrupt_pin(pdev->config, 1);
    /* The guest prefers MSI and falls back to INTx without it. */
    ret = msi_init(pdev, 0, 1, true, false, &err);
    if (ret == -ENOTSUP) {
        warn_reportf_err(err, "vaddress: using INTx: ");
    } else if (ret) {
        error_propagate(errp, err);
        return;
    }

    memory_region_init_io(&s->regs_mr, OBJECT(s), &va_regs_ops, s,
                          "vaddress_control", VADDRESS_REGS_SIZE);
    pci_register_bar(pdev, 0, PCI_BASE_ADDRESS_SPACE_MEMORY, &s->regs_mr);

    if (!memory_region_init_ram_flags_nomigrate(&s->control_mr, OBJECT(s),
                                                "vaddress_control_mem",
                                                VADDRESS_CONTROL_SIZE, 0,
                                                errp)) {
        return;
    }
    pci_register_bar(pdev, 1, PCI_BASE_ADDRESS_SPACE_MEMORY |
                     PCI_BASE_ADDRESS_MEM_PREFETCH, &s->control_mr);

    memory_region_init(&s->mem_mr, OBJECT(s), "vaddress_mem",
                       VADDRESS_MEM_SIZE);
    pci_register_bar(pdev, 2, PCI_BASE_ADDRESS_SPACE_MEMORY |
                     PCI_BASE_ADDRESS_MEM_TYPE_64 |
                     PCI_BASE_ADDRESS_MEM_PREFETCH, &s->mem_mr);

    if (s->renderd && s->gfx_lib) {
        error_setg(errp, "vaddress: renderd and gfx-lib are two ways to run the renderer: give one");
    } else if (s->renderd) {
        vaddress_renderd_init(DEVICE(s), s->renderd, s->gfx_width, s->gfx_height, errp);
    } else if (s->gfx_lib) {
        vaddress_gl_init(DEVICE(s), s->gfx_lib, s->gfx_width, s->gfx_height,
                         errp);
    }
}

static void va_exit(PCIDevice *pdev)
{
    va_free_all_blocks(VADDRESS(pdev));
    msi_uninit(pdev);
}

/* ponytail: block state is host-side and dynamic; add a vmstate (blocks +
 * BAR1 + RAM contents) when snapshots/migration are needed. */
static const VMStateDescription vmstate_vaddress = {
    .name = TYPE_VADDRESS,
    .unmigratable = 1,
};

static const Property vaddress_properties[] = {
    /* path to libkiln_gfx; unset = no GL (GL clients are refused) */
    DEFINE_PROP_STRING("gfx-lib", VAddressState, gfx_lib),
    /* or kiln-renderd's socket: the renderer in its own process (docs/PLAN-RENDERD.md) */
    DEFINE_PROP_STRING("renderd", VAddressState, renderd),
    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("gfx-width", VAddressState, gfx_width, 720),
    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("gfx-height", VAddressState, gfx_height, 1280),
};

static void va_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, const void *data)
{
    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
    PCIDeviceClass *k = PCI_DEVICE_CLASS(klass);

    k->realize = va_realize;
    k->exit = va_exit;
    k->vendor_id = VADDRESS_VENDOR_ID;
    k->device_id = VADDRESS_DEVICE_ID;
    k->revision = 0; /* the guest driver rejects any other revision */
    k->class_id = VADDRESS_CLASS_ID;
    device_class_set_legacy_reset(dc, va_reset);
    dc->vmsd = &vmstate_vaddress;
    device_class_set_props(dc, vaddress_properties);
    dc->desc = "Address space device";
    set_bit(DEVICE_CATEGORY_MISC, dc->categories);
}

static const TypeInfo vaddress_types[] = {
    {
        .name          = TYPE_VADDRESS,
        .parent        = TYPE_PCI_DEVICE,
        .instance_size = sizeof(VAddressState),
        .class_init    = va_class_init,
        .interfaces    = (const InterfaceInfo[]) {
            { INTERFACE_CONVENTIONAL_PCI_DEVICE },
            { },
        },
    },
    {
        .name          = TYPE_VADDRESS_HOSTMEM,
        .parent        = TYPE_OBJECT,
        .instance_size = sizeof(VAddressHostMem),
        .instance_finalize = va_hostmem_finalize,
    },
};

DEFINE_TYPES(vaddress_types)

```

`source/qemu/include/hw/misc/kiln-renderd.h`:

```h
/*
 * Control protocol between QEMU's vaddress device and kiln-renderd, the renderer in its own process
 * (docs/PLAN-RENDERD.md). One Unix stream socket per VM, fixed 24-byte messages; a shared-memory fd rides
 * along with BIND and REBIND (SCM_RIGHTS). The stream bytes never cross the socket: both sides map the
 * same ring block (vaddress-ring.h).
 *
 * Built into QEMU and into kiln-renderd (C++), hence the dual licence.
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later OR MIT
 */

#ifndef HW_MISC_KILN_RENDERD_H
#define HW_MISC_KILN_RENDERD_H

#include <errno.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <sys/types.h>

#define KRD_VERSION 1

enum {
    /* QEMU -> renderd */
    KRD_HELLO = 1,      /* a = version, b = width << 32 | height. Answered by HELLO {a = version, b = 1 if the renderer started} */
    KRD_BIND,           /* new stream; a = block size, fd = the block */
    KRD_REBIND,         /* the stream moved to a bigger block; a = size, fd = the new block */
    KRD_UNBIND,         /* the stream is gone; renderd stops using its block */
    KRD_KICK,           /* the guest rang the stream's doorbell */
    KRD_SYNC_PRESENT,   /* a = 1 when the guest bound the sync sub-device, 0 when it let go */
    KRD_SYNC_TRIGGER,   /* a = glsync, b = thread: one native sync work item */
    /* renderd -> QEMU */
    KRD_RAISE,          /* raise the stream's interrupt pin */
    KRD_SYNC_INC,       /* a = host fences passed */
};

typedef struct KrdMsg {
    uint32_t type;
    uint32_t stream;    /* the sub-device id, for the stream messages */
    uint64_t a, b;
} KrdMsg;

#ifdef __cplusplus
static_assert(sizeof(KrdMsg) == 24, "KrdMsg is wire format");
#else
_Static_assert(sizeof(KrdMsg) == 24, "KrdMsg is wire format");
#endif

/* Sends one message, with fd unless it is -1; flags as for sendmsg (MSG_DONTWAIT). Returns 0, or -1 with errno set. */
static inline int krd_send(int sock, const KrdMsg *m, int fd, int flags)
{
    union {
        struct cmsghdr h;
        char buf[CMSG_SPACE(sizeof(int))];
    } ctl;
    struct iovec iov = { (void *)m, sizeof(*m) };
    struct msghdr mh;
    ssize_t n;

    memset(&mh, 0, sizeof(mh));
    mh.msg_iov = &iov;
    mh.msg_iovlen = 1;
    if (fd >= 0) {
        memset(&ctl, 0, sizeof(ctl));
        mh.msg_control = ctl.buf;
        mh.msg_controllen = sizeof(ctl.buf);
        CMSG_FIRSTHDR(&mh)->cmsg_level = SOL_SOCKET;
        CMSG_FIRSTHDR(&mh)->cmsg_type = SCM_RIGHTS;
        CMSG_FIRSTHDR(&mh)->cmsg_len = CMSG_LEN(sizeof(int));
        memcpy(CMSG_DATA(CMSG_FIRSTHDR(&mh)), &fd, sizeof(int));
    }
    do {
        n = sendmsg(sock, &mh, flags);
    } while (n < 0 && errno == EINTR);
    if (n == (ssize_t)sizeof(*m)) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (n >= 0) {
        errno = EMSGSIZE; /* a short write of 24 bytes on a stream socket: the peer is going away */
    }
    return -1;
}

/* Receives one message (blocking); *fd is the fd that came with it, or -1. Returns 1, 0 at end of stream, -1 on error. */
static inline int krd_recv(int sock, KrdMsg *m, int *fd)
{
    union {
        struct cmsghdr h;
        char buf[CMSG_SPACE(sizeof(int))];
    } ctl;
    struct iovec iov = { m, sizeof(*m) };
    struct msghdr mh;
    struct cmsghdr *c;
    ssize_t n;

    memset(&mh, 0, sizeof(mh));
    mh.msg_iov = &iov;
    mh.msg_iovlen = 1;
    mh.msg_control = ctl.buf;
    mh.msg_controllen = sizeof(ctl.buf);
    *fd = -1;
    do {
        n = recvmsg(sock, &mh, MSG_WAITALL);
    } while (n < 0 && errno == EINTR);
    for (c = CMSG_FIRSTHDR(&mh); n > 0 && c; c = CMSG_NXTHDR(&mh, c)) {
        if (c->cmsg_level == SOL_SOCKET && c->cmsg_type == SCM_RIGHTS) {
            memcpy(fd, CMSG_DATA(c), sizeof(int));
        }
    }
    if (n == (ssize_t)sizeof(*m)) {
        return 1;
    }
    if (n >= 0) {
        return 0; /* end of stream, or a message cut short by it */
    }
    return -1;
}

/*
 * Host-visible memory (gfxstream's vm ops: Vulkan host-visible allocations, GlDirectMem, mapped ColorBuffers). The
 * renderer's pages must appear at a guest-physical address, so QEMU needs them in its own address space. A Mach memory
 * entry shares any range of renderd's memory, Metal's buffers included, so renderd sends one per map over a Mach port
 * QEMU checks in with launchd as KRD_VM_SERVICE "<QEMU pid>" (renderd finds the pid on the socket). Both calls are
 * synchronous, as in process: the guest touches the memory as soon as the renderer replies to it.
 */
#ifdef __APPLE__
#include <mach/mach.h>

#define KRD_VM_SERVICE "kiln.vaddress.vm."

enum {
    KRD_VM_MAP = 0x4b520001,   /* entry = the pages; map gpa..gpa+size to entry + offset */
    KRD_VM_UNMAP,              /* unmap gpa..gpa+size; no descriptor */
};

typedef struct KrdVmMsg {
    mach_msg_header_t h;       /* msgh_id: KRD_VM_MAP or KRD_VM_UNMAP */
    mach_msg_body_t body;
    mach_msg_port_descriptor_t entry;
    uint64_t gpa, size;
    uint64_t offset;           /* where the mapped pointer sits in the entry (which starts on a host page) */
    uint64_t entry_size;
} KrdVmMsg;

typedef struct KrdVmReply {
    mach_msg_header_t h;
    int32_t ok;
} KrdVmReply;
#endif

#endif

```

`source/qemu/include/hw/misc/vaddress-ring.h`:

```h
/*
 * Host side of the VAddressStream byte rings (kiln/docs/vaddress-guest.md
 * section 6). Self-contained so it can be unit tested without a device.
 *
 * Every header field lives in guest memory and may change under our feet,
 * so each one is read once, validated against the block size, and only the
 * validated copy is used.
 *
 * Also built into kiln-renderd (C++, outside QEMU), hence the dual licence and
 * the MIN fallback.
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later OR MIT
 */

#ifndef HW_MISC_VADDRESS_RING_H
#define HW_MISC_VADDRESS_RING_H

#ifndef MIN
#define MIN(a, b) ((a) < (b) ? (a) : (b))
#endif

#define VAS_HEADER_SIZE         0x1000
#define VAS_HOST_STATE          0x008
#define VAS_NAME                0x014
#define VAS_NAME_SIZE           44
#define VAS_GUEST_WRITE_STATE   0x040
#define VAS_GUEST_READ_STATE    0x044
#define VAS_TO_HOST             0x080 /* guest -> host ring descriptor */
#define VAS_FROM_HOST           0x100 /* host -> guest ring descriptor */

/* Ring descriptor field offsets */
#define VAS_RING_DATA_OFFSET    0x00
#define VAS_RING_SIZE           0x04
#define VAS_RING_WRITE_POS      0x10
#define VAS_RING_WRAP_POS       0x14
#define VAS_RING_READ_POS       0x40

/* guest_write_state: (n << 2) | 3 = writer blocked; guest_read_state: 2 */
#define VAS_WRITER_BLOCKED(s)   (((s) & 3) == 3)
#define VAS_READER_BLOCKED      2

/* Header fields are little-endian u32. */
static inline uint32_t vas_load(const uint8_t *blk, size_t off)
{
    uint32_t v = __atomic_load_n((const uint32_t *)(blk + off),
                                 __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
#if __BYTE_ORDER__ == __ORDER_BIG_ENDIAN__
    v = __builtin_bswap32(v);
#endif
    return v;
}

static inline void vas_store(uint8_t *blk, size_t off, uint32_t val)
{
#if __BYTE_ORDER__ == __ORDER_BIG_ENDIAN__
    val = __builtin_bswap32(val);
#endif
    __atomic_store_n((uint32_t *)(blk + off), val, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
}

/* Is a guest thread about to sleep (PING 0x76) waiting for the host? */
static inline bool vas_guest_waiting(const uint8_t *blk)
{
    return VAS_WRITER_BLOCKED(vas_load(blk, VAS_GUEST_WRITE_STATE)) ||
           vas_load(blk, VAS_GUEST_READ_STATE) == VAS_READER_BLOCKED;
}

/* Is the guest reader asleep (or about to be) on the from-host ring? */
static inline bool vas_reader_blocked(const uint8_t *blk)
{
    return vas_load(blk, VAS_GUEST_READ_STATE) == VAS_READER_BLOCKED;
}

typedef struct VASRing {
    uint8_t *data;
    uint32_t size;
} VASRing;

/* Validate the descriptor at @desc; false if the guest set it up wrongly. */
static inline bool vas_ring_get(uint8_t *blk, size_t blk_size, size_t desc,
                                VASRing *r)
{
    uint32_t off = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_DATA_OFFSET);
    uint32_t size = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_SIZE);

    if (blk_size < VAS_HEADER_SIZE || size < 2 || off < VAS_HEADER_SIZE ||
        off > blk_size || size > blk_size - off) {
        return false;
    }
    r->data = blk + off;
    r->size = size;
    return true;
}

/*
 * Zero-copy reading of the to-host ring: the contiguous unread bytes start at *@data (0 when the ring is empty, -1 if
 * its state is corrupt); vas_ring_consume() then frees @n of them, at most what vas_ring_peek() returned.
 */
static inline ssize_t vas_ring_peek(uint8_t *blk, size_t blk_size, uint8_t **data)
{
    size_t desc = VAS_TO_HOST;
    VASRing r;

    if (!vas_ring_get(blk, blk_size, desc, &r)) {
        return -1;
    }
    uint32_t wp = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS);
    uint32_t wrap = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_WRAP_POS);
    uint32_t rp = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_READ_POS);

    if (wp >= r.size || rp >= r.size || wrap > r.size) {
        return -1;
    }
    if (wp < rp && wrap <= rp) {
        /* wp < rp: data is [rp, wrap) then [0, wp); the tail is used up */
        rp = 0;
        vas_store(blk, desc + VAS_RING_READ_POS, rp);
    }
    if (rp == wp) {
        return 0;
    }
    *data = r.data + rp;
    return (wp > rp ? wp : wrap) - rp;
}

static inline void vas_ring_consume(uint8_t *blk, size_t blk_size, size_t n)
{
    size_t desc = VAS_TO_HOST;
    uint32_t rp = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_READ_POS) + n; /* the host's own field */
    uint32_t wp = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS);

    if (wp < rp && rp == vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_WRAP_POS)) {
        rp = 0;
    }
    vas_store(blk, desc + VAS_RING_READ_POS, rp);
}

/*
 * Consume up to @len bytes of the to-host ring into @buf.
 * Returns bytes read, or -1 if the ring state is corrupt.
 */
static inline ssize_t vas_ring_read(uint8_t *blk, size_t blk_size,
                                    void *buf, size_t len)
{
    size_t total = 0;

    while (total < len) {
        uint8_t *data;
        ssize_t n = vas_ring_peek(blk, blk_size, &data);

        if (n < 0) {
            return total ? (ssize_t)total : -1;
        }
        if (n == 0) {
            break;
        }
        n = MIN((size_t)n, len - total);
        memcpy((uint8_t *)buf + total, data, n);
        vas_ring_consume(blk, blk_size, n);
        total += n;
    }
    return total;
}

/* Free bytes in the from-host ring, or -1 if the ring state is corrupt. */
static inline ssize_t vas_ring_room(uint8_t *blk, size_t blk_size)
{
    VASRing r;

    if (!vas_ring_get(blk, blk_size, VAS_FROM_HOST, &r)) {
        return -1;
    }
    uint32_t wp = vas_load(blk, VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS);
    uint32_t rp = vas_load(blk, VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_READ_POS);

    if (wp >= r.size || rp >= r.size) {
        return -1;
    }
    return wp < rp ? rp - wp - 1 : r.size - wp + rp - 1;
}

/* Does the from-host ring hold bytes the guest has not consumed yet? */
static inline bool vas_ring_unread(uint8_t *blk, size_t blk_size)
{
    VASRing r;

    if (!vas_ring_get(blk, blk_size, VAS_FROM_HOST, &r)) {
        return false;
    }
    return vas_load(blk, VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS) !=
           vas_load(blk, VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_READ_POS);
}

/*
 * Zero-copy writing of the from-host ring, mirroring the guest producer (one slot kept empty, wrap_pos marks the end
 * of valid data): the contiguous free bytes start at *@data (0 when the ring is full, -1 if its state is corrupt).
 * Fill them, then vas_ring_commit() publishes @n of them, at most what vas_ring_reserve() returned.
 */
static inline ssize_t vas_ring_reserve(uint8_t *blk, size_t blk_size, uint8_t **data)
{
    size_t desc = VAS_FROM_HOST;
    VASRing r;

    if (!vas_ring_get(blk, blk_size, desc, &r)) {
        return -1;
    }
    uint32_t wp = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS);
    uint32_t rp = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_READ_POS);

    if (wp >= r.size || rp >= r.size) {
        return -1;
    }
    *data = r.data + wp;
    return wp < rp ? rp - wp - 1 : r.size - wp - (rp == 0);
}

static inline void vas_ring_commit(uint8_t *blk, size_t blk_size, size_t n)
{
    size_t desc = VAS_FROM_HOST;
    VASRing r;

    if (!vas_ring_get(blk, blk_size, desc, &r)) {
        return; /* the guest broke the header since the reserve: the next one reports it */
    }
    uint32_t wp = vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS); /* the host's own field */

    /* The reader cannot pass the writer, so this test cannot turn false since the reserve; if the reader wrapped to 0
     * meanwhile, wrap_pos is set as a fresh write would set it. */
    if (wp >= vas_load(blk, desc + VAS_RING_READ_POS)) {
        vas_store(blk, desc + VAS_RING_WRAP_POS, wp + n);
    }
    wp += n;
    if (wp == r.size) {
        wp = 0;
    }
    /* publish each step: the guest may read the tail before the head */
    vas_store(blk, desc + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS, wp);
}

/*
 * Produce up to @len bytes into the from-host ring.
 * Returns bytes written, or -1 if the ring state is corrupt.
 */
static inline ssize_t vas_ring_write(uint8_t *blk, size_t blk_size,
                                     const void *buf, size_t len)
{
    size_t total = 0;

    while (total < len) {
        uint8_t *data;
        ssize_t n = vas_ring_reserve(blk, blk_size, &data);

        if (n < 0) {
            return total ? (ssize_t)total : -1;
        }
        if (n == 0) {
            break;
        }
        n = MIN((size_t)n, len - total);
        memcpy(data, (const uint8_t *)buf + total, n);
        vas_ring_commit(blk, blk_size, n);
        total += n;
    }
    return total;
}

#endif

```

`source/qemu/include/hw/misc/vaddress.h`:

```h
/*
 * vaddress sub-device interface (kiln).
 *
 * A sub-device instance is created by the guest's BIND command on a mapped
 * block and lives until TERMINATE, unmap or reset. All callbacks run with the
 * BQL held, in the vCPU thread that touched the device, so block memory stays
 * valid for their whole duration.
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
 */

#ifndef HW_MISC_VADDRESS_H
#define HW_MISC_VADDRESS_H

typedef struct VAddressState VAddressState;
typedef struct VAddressSubDevice VAddressSubDevice;

typedef struct VAddressSubDeviceOps {
    uint32_t type;
    /* Optional. New instance on sd->mem; false refuses the BIND. */
    bool (*bind)(VAddressSubDevice *sd);
    /* Optional. The instance moves to a bigger block (BIND with an old block); BQL held. Must switch sd->mem and
     * sd->size atomically with respect to any thread using them, and not touch the old memory after returning. */
    void (*rebind)(VAddressSubDevice *sd, uint8_t *mem, uint64_t size);
    /* Guest rang the doorbell. */
    void (*notify)(VAddressSubDevice *sd);
    /* Optional. Instance is going away; free sd->opaque. */
    void (*unbind)(VAddressSubDevice *sd);
} VAddressSubDeviceOps;

struct VAddressSubDevice {
    VAddressState *dev;
    const VAddressSubDeviceOps *ops;
    void *opaque;
    uint32_t id;      /* doorbell value */
    uint32_t pin;     /* interrupt pin index, 0..31 */
    /* Host view of the bound block. Changes when the guest moves the
     * instance to a bigger block (BIND with an old block). */
    uint8_t *mem;
    uint64_t size;
    int fd;           /* shared memory behind mem (renderd mode), else -1 */
};

/* Wake the guest threads sleeping on this instance's pin. */
void vaddress_raise_pin(VAddressSubDevice *sd);

/* Map/unmap host memory (gfxstream's vm ops) at a block the guest ALLOCATEd; BQL held. own (may be NULL) is a mapping
 * of own_size bytes holding hva that QEMU unmaps once the guest no longer sees it; it stays the caller's on failure. */
bool vaddress_host_map(uint64_t gpa, void *hva, uint64_t size, void *own, uint64_t own_size);
void vaddress_host_unmap(uint64_t gpa, uint64_t size);

/* Sub-device 0x9c08 (guest vaddress_sample): byte-stream loopback. */
extern const VAddressSubDeviceOps vaddress_echo_ops;

/* Guest GL and Vulkan streams: upstream gfxstream protocol, served by libkiln_gfx once loaded. */
#define VADDRESS_SUBDEV_GFXSTREAM 0x6701
extern const VAddressSubDeviceOps vaddress_gfxstream_ops;
bool vaddress_gl_init(DeviceState *dev, const char *path, uint32_t width,
                      uint32_t height, Error **errp);
/* The same streams served by kiln-renderd, a separate process, over the socket at path (kiln-renderd.h). */
bool vaddress_renderd_init(DeviceState *dev, const char *path, uint32_t width,
                           uint32_t height, Error **errp);

/* GPU fence timeline for the guest's native sync (vaddress.md, "Sync sub-device"); served by libkiln_gfx too. */
#define VADDRESS_SUBDEV_SYNC 0x5f01
extern const VAddressSubDeviceOps vaddress_sync_ops;

#endif

```

```patch
+/*
+ * KILN_HVF_WFI_POLL_US=n: before a vCPU halts for WFI, look for work for up to n microseconds instead of sleeping at once. A
+ * wake-up from the halt costs a futex wake and a thread switch; guests that sleep and wake all the time (a game's threads)
+ * feel it. 0 (the default) halts at once. It costs host CPU while the guest is idle.
+ */
+static int64_t hvf_wfi_poll_ns(void)
+{
+    static int64_t ns = -1;
+
+    if (ns < 0) {
+        const char *v = getenv("KILN_HVF_WFI_POLL_US");
+        ns = v ? MAX(atoll(v), 0) * 1000 : 0;
+    }
+    return ns;
+}
+
+/* Spin without the BQL (the main loop raises the interrupts a halted vCPU waits for); true when work arrived. */
+static bool hvf_wfi_poll(CPUState *cpu)
+{
+    const int64_t poll_ns = hvf_wfi_poll_ns();
+    int64_t end;
+
+    if (!poll_ns) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    end = qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + poll_ns;
+    bql_unlock();
+    while (!cpu_has_work(cpu) && qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) < end) {
+        cpu_relax();
+    }
+    bql_lock();
+    return cpu_has_work(cpu);
+}
+
 static int hvf_wfi(CPUState *cpu)
 {
     if (cpu_has_work(cpu)) {
@@ -2280,6 +2314,10 @@ static int hvf_wfi(CPUState *cpu)
         return 0;
     }
 
+    if (hvf_wfi_poll(cpu)) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
     if (!hvf_irqchip_in_kernel()) {
         uint64_t ctl, cval;
         hv_return_t r;
```

`source/qemu/patches/0005-virt-kiln-cpu-layout.patch`:

```patch
--- a/hw/arm/virt.c
+++ b/hw/arm/virt.c
@@ -634,6 +634,50 @@
     return false;
 }
 
+/*
+ * Kiln: a phone's CPU layout in the device tree. "kiln-cpus" lists the clusters as <cores>:<max kHz>, joined by '/', little
+ * first (e.g. 6:2002000/2:2400000); their cores add up to -smp. The guest learns the clusters from cpu-map, and its
+ * kiln-cpufreq driver publishes "kiln,max-freq-khz" as each core's frequency. No capacity-dmips-mhz: the vCPUs are alike,
+ * and with a phone's capacities the guest's scheduler crowded heavy work onto the one "big" vCPU (Genshin lost frames).
+ */
+static void kiln_fdt_cpu_layout(VirtMachineState *vms)
+{
+    MachineState *ms = MACHINE(vms);
+    unsigned int cores[16], khz[16], n = 0, total = 0, cpu = 0;
+
+    if (!vms->kiln_cpus || !*vms->kiln_cpus) {
+        return;
+    }
+    g_auto(GStrv) clusters = g_strsplit(vms->kiln_cpus, "/", 0);
+    for (; clusters[n]; n++) {
+        if (n == ARRAY_SIZE(cores) ||
+            sscanf(clusters[n], "%u:%u", &cores[n], &khz[n]) != 2 || !cores[n] || !khz[n]) {
+            error_setg(&error_fatal, "kiln-cpus: bad cluster '%s' (<cores>:<kHz>)",
+                       clusters[n] ? clusters[n] : "");
+            return;
+        }
+        total += cores[n];
+    }
+    if (total != ms->smp.cpus) {
+        error_setg(&error_fatal, "kiln-cpus: %u cores, but -smp has %u", total, ms->smp.cpus);
+        return;
+    }
+    qemu_fdt_nop_node(ms->fdt, "/cpus/cpu-map");  /* the generic one: every vCPU in one cluster */
+    qemu_fdt_add_subnode(ms->fdt, "/cpus/cpu-map");
+    qemu_fdt_add_subnode(ms->fdt, "/cpus/cpu-map/socket0");
+    for (unsigned int c = 0; c < n; c++) {
+        g_autofree char *cluster = g_strdup_printf("/cpus/cpu-map/socket0/cluster%u", c);
+        qemu_fdt_add_subnode(ms->fdt, cluster);
+        for (unsigned int i = 0; i < cores[c]; i++, cpu++) {
+            g_autofree char *core = g_strdup_printf("%s/core%u", cluster, i);
+            g_autofree char *node = g_strdup_printf("/cpus/cpu@%u", cpu);
+            qemu_fdt_add_subnode(ms->fdt, core);
+            qemu_fdt_setprop_cell(ms->fdt, core, "cpu", vms->cpu_phandles[cpu]);
+            qemu_fdt_setprop_cell(ms->fdt, node, "kiln,max-freq-khz", khz[c]);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
 static void fdt_add_cpu_nodes(VirtMachineState *vms)
 {
     int cpu;
@@ -3153,6 +3197,7 @@
 
     fdt_add_timer_nodes(vms);
     fdt_add_cpu_nodes(vms);
+    kiln_fdt_cpu_layout(vms);
 
     memory_region_add_subregion(sysmem, vms->memmap[VIRT_MEM].base,
                                 machine->ram);
@@ -3406,6 +3451,19 @@
```

`source/qemu/tests/qtest/vaddress-test.c`:

```c
/*
 * QTest for the vaddress PCI device (kiln).
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
 */

#include "qemu/osdep.h"
#include "qemu/host-utils.h"
#include "qemu/bswap.h"
#include "libqtest.h"
#include "libqos/pci.h"
#include "libqos/generic-pcihost.h"
#include "hw/pci/pci_regs.h"
#include "hw/misc/vaddress-ring.h"
#include "hw/misc/kiln-renderd.h"
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/un.h>
#include <mach/mach_vm.h>
#include <servers/bootstrap.h>

#define REG_COMMAND 0x00
#define REG_STATUS  0x04
#define REG_ARG0    0x08
#define REG_ARG1    0x10
#define REG_ARG2    0x18
#define REG_ARG3    0x20
#define REG_ARG4    0x28
#define REG_DOORBELL 0x30

/* High PCIe MMIO window of the virt machine; the qtest allocator is 32-bit. */
#define BAR2_GPA    0x8000000000ULL

typedef struct {
    QTestState *qts;
    QGenericPCIBus gbus;
    QPCIDevice *dev;
    QPCIBar bar0, bar1;
} VA;

static void save_fn(QPCIDevice *dev, int devfn, void *data)
{
    *(QPCIDevice **)data = dev;
}

static void wr64(VA *va, uint64_t off, uint64_t v)
{
    qpci_io_writel(va->dev, va->bar0, off, v);
    qpci_io_writel(va->dev, va->bar0, off + 4, v >> 32);
}

static uint64_t rd64(VA *va, uint64_t off)
{
    return qpci_io_readl(va->dev, va->bar0, off) |
           (uint64_t)qpci_io_readl(va->dev, va->bar0, off + 4) << 32;
}

static uint32_t cmd(VA *va, uint32_t c)
{
    qpci_io_writel(va->dev, va->bar0, REG_COMMAND, c);
    return qpci_io_readl(va->dev, va->bar0, REG_STATUS);
}

static uint64_t allocate(VA *va, uint64_t size, uint64_t *actual)
{
    wr64(va, REG_ARG0, size);
    g_assert_cmpuint(cmd(va, 0), ==, 0);
    *actual = rd64(va, REG_ARG0);
    return rd64(va, REG_ARG1);
}

static uint32_t map(VA *va, uint64_t off, uint64_t hint, uint64_t size)
{
    wr64(va, REG_ARG0, off);
    wr64(va, REG_ARG1, hint);
    wr64(va, REG_ARG2, size);
    return cmd(va, 2);
}

static uint32_t by_offset(VA *va, uint32_t c, uint64_t off)
{
    wr64(va, REG_ARG0, off);
    return cmd(va, c);
}

static void dev_start_with(VA *va, const char *device)
{
    va->qts = qtest_initf("-machine virt,acpi=off,highmem=on "
                          "-cpu max -m 256 -net none -device %s", device);
    qpci_init_generic(&va->gbus, va->qts, NULL, false);
    qpci_device_foreach(&va->gbus.bus, 0x1c87, 0x1074, save_fn, &va->dev);
    g_assert(va->dev);
    qpci_device_enable(va->dev);
    va->bar0 = qpci_iomap(va->dev, 0, NULL);
    va->bar1 = qpci_iomap(va->dev, 1, NULL);
    qpci_config_writel(va->dev, PCI_BASE_ADDRESS_2, (uint32_t)BAR2_GPA);
    qpci_config_writel(va->dev, PCI_BASE_ADDRESS_3, BAR2_GPA >> 32);
}

static void dev_start(VA *va)
{
    dev_start_with(va, "vaddress");
}

static void dev_stop(VA *va)
{
    g_free(va->dev);
    qtest_quit(va->qts);
}

static void test_vaddress(void)
{
    VA va = { 0 };
    QTestState *qts;
    uint64_t off0, off1, size0, size1;
    uint8_t buf[64], out[64];

    dev_start(&va);
    qts = va.qts;

    /* Identity the guest driver checks. */
    g_assert_cmpuint(qpci_config_readb(va.dev, PCI_REVISION_ID), ==, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(qpci_config_readw(va.dev, PCI_CLASS_DEVICE), ==, 0x0500);
    g_assert_cmpuint(qpci_config_readb(va.dev, PCI_INTERRUPT_PIN), ==, 1);

    /* SET_PARAM: value straddles 0x0c..0x13. */
    qpci_io_writel(va.dev, va.bar0, REG_ARG0, 0);
    wr64(&va, REG_ARG0 + 4, BAR2_GPA);
    g_assert_cmpuint(cmd(&va, 9), ==, 0);
    qpci_io_writel(va.dev, va.bar0, REG_ARG0, 1);
    wr64(&va, REG_ARG0 + 4, 1);
    g_assert_cmpuint(cmd(&va, 9), !=, 0);

    /* ALLOCATE rounds to the host page and packs blocks first-fit. */
    off0 = allocate(&va, 0x1234, &size0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(off0, ==, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(size0, >=, 0x1234);
    g_assert_cmpuint(size0 % 4096, ==, 0);
    off1 = allocate(&va, 1, &size1);
    g_assert_cmpuint(off1, ==, size0);
    wr64(&va, REG_ARG0, 16ULL << 30);
    g_assert_cmpuint(cmd(&va, 0), !=, 0);

    /* MAP_HVA with host allocation makes RAM appear in BAR2. */
    g_assert_cmpuint(map(&va, off0, 0, 0x1234), ==, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(rd64(&va, REG_ARG1), !=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(rd64(&va, REG_ARG2), ==, size0);
    for (int i = 0; i < sizeof(buf); i++) {
        buf[i] = i * 7;
    }
    qtest_memwrite(qts, BAR2_GPA + off0 + 0x100, buf, sizeof(buf));
    qtest_memread(qts, BAR2_GPA + off0 + 0x100, out, sizeof(out));
    g_assert_cmpmem(buf, sizeof(buf), out, sizeof(out));

    /* Refusals: double map, remote/host-VA hints, oversize, unknown block. */
    g_assert_cmpuint(map(&va, off0, 0, 0x1000), !=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(map(&va, off1, 1, 0x1000), !=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(map(&va, off1, 0x12345000, 0x1000), !=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(map(&va, off1, 0, size1 + 1), !=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(map(&va, 0x7777000, 0, 0x1000), !=, 0);

    /* No sub-device backend yet: BIND must fail cleanly. */
    wr64(&va, REG_ARG0, off0);
    qpci_io_writel(va.dev, va.bar0, REG_ARG4, 0x6701);
    g_assert_cmpuint(cmd(&va, 5), !=, 0);

    /* Invalid commands. */
    g_assert_cmpuint(cmd(&va, 4), !=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(cmd(&va, 7), !=, 0);

    /* UNMAP, DEALLOCATE (also of a still-mapped block), then reuse. */
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 3, off0), ==, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 3, off0), !=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(map(&va, off0, 0, size0), ==, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 1, off0), ==, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 1, off0), !=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(allocate(&va, 0x1000, &size0), ==, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(map(&va, 0, 0, size0), ==, 0);

    /* System reset drops every block. */
    qtest_system_reset(qts);
    qpci_device_enable(va.dev);
    va.bar0 = qpci_iomap(va.dev, 0, NULL);
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 1, 0), !=, 0);

    dev_stop(&va);
}

#define FH 0x400 /* from-host ring size (init daemon) */
#define TH 0x400 /* to-host ring size */

static void wr32(VA *va, uint64_t gpa, uint32_t v)
{
    qtest_writel(va->qts, gpa, v);
}

static uint32_t rd32(VA *va, uint64_t gpa)
{
    return qtest_readl(va->qts, gpa);
}

/* Guest steps 2-4 of a VAddressStream: allocate, map, init the header. */
static uint64_t stream_block(VA *va)
{
    uint64_t total = VAS_HEADER_SIZE + FH + TH, size, off, h;

    off = allocate(va, total, &size);
    g_assert_cmpuint(map(va, off, 0, total), ==, 0);
    h = BAR2_GPA + off;
    wr32(va, h + VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_DATA_OFFSET, VAS_HEADER_SIZE + FH);
    wr32(va, h + VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_SIZE, TH);
    wr32(va, h + VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_DATA_OFFSET, VAS_HEADER_SIZE);
    wr32(va, h + VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_SIZE, FH);
    wr32(va, h + VAS_GUEST_WRITE_STATE, 1);
    wr32(va, h + VAS_GUEST_READ_STATE, 1);
    return off;
}

static uint32_t bind_sd(VA *va, uint64_t new_off, uint64_t old_off,
                     uint32_t type, uint32_t *pin_mask)
{
    uint32_t st;

    wr64(va, REG_ARG0, new_off);
    wr64(va, REG_ARG3, old_off);
    qpci_io_writel(va->dev, va->bar0, REG_ARG4, type);
    st = cmd(va, 5);
    *pin_mask = qpci_io_readl(va->dev, va->bar0, REG_ARG1);
    return st ? 0 : qpci_io_readl(va->dev, va->bar0, REG_ARG0);
}

/* Guest writes @msg into the to-host ring and rings the doorbell. */
static void send_msg(VA *va, uint64_t off, uint32_t id, const char *msg)
{
    uint64_t h = BAR2_GPA + off;
    uint32_t wp = rd32(va, h + VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS);
    size_t n = strlen(msg);

    qtest_memwrite(va->qts, h + VAS_HEADER_SIZE + FH + wp, msg, n);
    wr32(va, h + VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_WRAP_POS, wp + n);
    wr32(va, h + VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS, wp + n);
    qpci_io_writel(va->dev, va->bar0, REG_DOORBELL, id);
}

static void expect_echo(VA *va, uint64_t off, uint32_t from, const char *msg)
{
    uint64_t h = BAR2_GPA + off;
    size_t n = strlen(msg);
    char got[64] = { 0 };

    g_assert_cmpuint(rd32(va, h + VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_READ_POS), ==,
                     rd32(va, h + VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS));
    g_assert_cmpuint(rd32(va, h + VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS), ==,
                     from + n);
    qtest_memread(va->qts, h + VAS_HEADER_SIZE + from, got, n);
    g_assert_cmpstr(got, ==, msg);
}

static void test_echo(void)
{
    VA va = { 0 };
    uint32_t id, id2, pin, pin2, w;
    uint64_t a, b;

    dev_start(&va);

    /* Unknown sub-device types are refused. */
    a = stream_block(&va);
    g_assert_cmpuint(bind_sd(&va, a, a, 0x6701, &pin), ==, 0);

    /* BIND 0x9c08: doorbell id and a one-bit pin mask come back. */
    id = bind_sd(&va, a, a, 0x9c08, &pin);
    g_assert_cmpuint(id, !=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpuint(ctpop32(pin), ==, 1);
    g_assert_cmpuint(bind_sd(&va, a, a, 0x9c08, &pin2), ==, 0); /* already bound */

    /* Reader asleep: the echo lands and the pin is queued in BAR1. */
    wr32(&va, BAR2_GPA + a + VAS_GUEST_READ_STATE, VAS_READER_BLOCKED);
    send_msg(&va, a, id, "hello");
    expect_echo(&va, a, 0, "hello");
    w = qpci_io_readl(va.dev, va.bar1, 0x20);
    g_assert_cmpuint(w, ==, 1);
    g_assert_cmpuint(qpci_io_readb(va.dev, va.bar1, 0), ==, ctz32(pin));

    /* Same pin still pending: the entry is pushed again (never skipped), so a sleeping guest
     * reader cannot miss a wakeup; the guest drains duplicates. */
    send_msg(&va, a, id, "again");
    expect_echo(&va, a, 5, "again");
    g_assert_cmpuint(qpci_io_readl(va.dev, va.bar1, 0x20), ==, 2);

    /* Ring resize: the instance moves to the new block, same id. */
    b = stream_block(&va);
    id2 = bind_sd(&va, b, a, 0x9c08, &pin2);
    g_assert_cmpuint(id2, ==, id);
    g_assert_cmpuint(pin2, ==, pin);
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 1, a), ==, 0);
    send_msg(&va, b, id, "moved");
    expect_echo(&va, b, 0, "moved");

    /* TERMINATE: the doorbell no longer reaches anything. */
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 8, b), ==, 0);
    send_msg(&va, b, id, "gone");
    g_assert_cmpuint(rd32(&va, BAR2_GPA + b + VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_READ_POS),
                     ==, 5);

    /* A corrupt header is rejected without touching host memory. */
    id = bind_sd(&va, b, b, 0x9c08, &pin);
    g_assert_cmpuint(id, !=, 0);
    wr32(&va, BAR2_GPA + b + VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_SIZE, 0x7fffffff);
    send_msg(&va, b, id, "x");

    /* Unmapping a bound block tears the instance down first. */
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 3, b), ==, 0);
    qpci_io_writel(va.dev, va.bar0, REG_DOORBELL, id);

    dev_stop(&va);
}

/* The test plays kiln-renderd: it listens, and answers QEMU's HELLO while qtest_init waits for the device. */
typedef struct {
    char *path;
    int lst, conn;
} FakeRenderd;

static gpointer fake_renderd_hello(gpointer opaque)
{
    FakeRenderd *fr = opaque;
    KrdMsg m;
    int fd;

    fr->conn = accept(fr->lst, NULL, NULL);
    g_assert_cmpint(fr->conn, >=, 0);
    g_assert_cmpint(krd_recv(fr->conn, &m, &fd), ==, 1);
    g_assert_cmpuint(m.type, ==, KRD_HELLO);
    g_assert_cmpuint(m.a, ==, KRD_VERSION);
    g_assert_cmpuint(m.b, ==, (720ULL << 32) | 1280);
    m.b = 1; /* the renderer started */
    g_assert_cmpint(krd_send(fr->conn, &m, -1, 0), ==, 0);
    return NULL;
}

static KrdMsg expect_msg(FakeRenderd *fr, uint32_t type, uint32_t stream, int *fd)
{
    KrdMsg m;
    int got;

    g_assert_cmpint(krd_recv(fr->conn, &m, &got), ==, 1);
    g_assert_cmpuint(m.type, ==, type);
    g_assert_cmpuint(m.stream, ==, stream);
    if (fd) {
        *fd = got;
    } else {
        g_assert_cmpint(got, ==, -1);
    }
    return m;
}

/* A vm ops call as renderd makes it (kiln-renderd.h): returns QEMU's ok. */
static int32_t vm_call(mach_port_t port, KrdVmMsg *m)
{
    union {
        KrdVmMsg m;
        struct {
            KrdVmReply r;
            mach_msg_trailer_t trailer;
        } reply;
    } buf = { .m = *m };

    buf.m.h.msgh_bits = MACH_MSGH_BITS(MACH_MSG_TYPE_COPY_SEND, MACH_MSG_TYPE_MAKE_SEND_ONCE) | MACH_MSGH_BITS_COMPLEX;
    buf.m.h.msgh_size = sizeof(KrdVmMsg);
    buf.m.h.msgh_remote_port = port;
    buf.m.h.msgh_local_port = mig_get_reply_port();
    g_assert_cmpint(mach_msg(&buf.m.h, MACH_SEND_MSG | MACH_RCV_MSG, sizeof(KrdVmMsg), sizeof(buf),
                             buf.m.h.msgh_local_port, MACH_MSG_TIMEOUT_NONE, MACH_PORT_NULL), ==, KERN_SUCCESS);
    return buf.reply.r.ok;
}

/* Renderer memory (here the test's own page) appears in BAR2 at a block the guest allocated, both ways. */
static void check_vm_ops(VA *va, FakeRenderd *fr)
{
    g_autofree char *name = NULL;
    mach_port_t port, entry;
    memory_object_size_t esz = vm_page_size;
    socklen_t len = sizeof(pid_t);
    pid_t qemu = 0;
    uint64_t off, size;
    KrdVmMsg m = { 0 };
    char *page, got[16] = { 0 };

    g_assert_cmpint(getsockopt(fr->conn, SOL_LOCAL, LOCAL_PEERPID, &qemu, &len), ==, 0);
    name = g_strdup_printf(KRD_VM_SERVICE "%d", (int)qemu);
    g_assert_cmpint(bootstrap_look_up(bootstrap_port, name, &port), ==, KERN_SUCCESS);

    qpci_io_writel(va->dev, va->bar0, REG_ARG0, 0); /* SET_PARAM(MEM_BAR_GPA) */
    wr64(va, REG_ARG0 + 4, BAR2_GPA);
    g_assert_cmpuint(cmd(va, 9), ==, 0);
    off = allocate(va, vm_page_size, &size);

    page = mmap(NULL, vm_page_size, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_ANON | MAP_PRIVATE, -1, 0);
    g_assert(page != MAP_FAILED);
    strcpy(page + 0x40, "renderer");
    g_assert_cmpint(mach_make_memory_entry_64(mach_task_self(), &esz, (mach_vm_offset_t)page,
                                              VM_PROT_READ | VM_PROT_WRITE | MAP_MEM_VM_SHARE, &entry,
                                              MACH_PORT_NULL), ==, KERN_SUCCESS);
    m.h.msgh_id = KRD_VM_MAP;
    m.body.msgh_descriptor_count = 1;
    m.entry = (mach_msg_port_descriptor_t){ .name = entry, .disposition = MACH_MSG_TYPE_MOVE_SEND,
                                            .type = MACH_MSG_PORT_DESCRIPTOR };
    m.gpa = BAR2_GPA + off;
    m.size = vm_page_size;
    m.entry_size = esz;
    g_assert_cmpint(vm_call(port, &m), ==, 1);
    qtest_memread(va->qts, BAR2_GPA + off + 0x40, got, 9);
    g_assert_cmpstr(got, ==, "renderer");
    qtest_memwrite(va->qts, BAR2_GPA + off + 0x80, "guest", 6);
    g_assert_cmpstr(page + 0x80, ==, "guest");

    /* A second map of the same block is refused (QEMU drops the entry it was sent). */
    g_assert_cmpint(mach_make_memory_entry_64(mach_task_self(), &esz, (mach_vm_offset_t)page,
                                              VM_PROT_READ | VM_PROT_WRITE | MAP_MEM_VM_SHARE, &entry,
                                              MACH_PORT_NULL), ==, KERN_SUCCESS);
    m.entry.name = entry;
    g_assert_cmpint(vm_call(port, &m), ==, 0);

    memset(&m, 0, sizeof(m));
    m.h.msgh_id = KRD_VM_UNMAP;
    m.gpa = BAR2_GPA + off;
    m.size = vm_page_size;
    g_assert_cmpint(vm_call(port, &m), ==, 1);
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(va, 1, off), ==, 0); /* the block is free of renderer memory: DEALLOCATE works */
    munmap(page, vm_page_size);
}

static void test_renderd(void)
{
    FakeRenderd fr;
    struct sockaddr_un addr = { .sun_family = AF_UNIX };
    g_autofree char *device = NULL;
    VA va = { 0 };
    GThread *t;
    KrdMsg m;
    uint64_t a, b, size;
    uint32_t id, pin, pin2;
    uint8_t *shm;
    char got[8] = { 0 };
    int fd, i;

    fr.path = g_strdup_printf("%s/kiln-renderd-test-%d.sock", g_get_tmp_dir(), (int)getpid());
    g_assert_cmpuint(strlen(fr.path), <, sizeof(addr.sun_path));
    strcpy(addr.sun_path, fr.path);
    unlink(fr.path);
    fr.lst = socket(AF_UNIX, SOCK_STREAM, 0);
    g_assert_cmpint(bind(fr.lst, (struct sockaddr *)&addr, sizeof(addr)), ==, 0);
    g_assert_cmpint(listen(fr.lst, 1), ==, 0);
    t = g_thread_new("fake-renderd", fake_renderd_hello, &fr);
    device = g_strdup_printf("vaddress,renderd=%s", fr.path);
    dev_start_with(&va, device);
    g_thread_join(t);
    check_vm_ops(&va, &fr);

    /* BIND hands renderd the block as shared memory: its mapping sees the header the guest wrote. */
    a = stream_block(&va);
    id = bind_sd(&va, a, a, 0x6701, &pin);
    g_assert_cmpuint(id, !=, 0);
    m = expect_msg(&fr, KRD_BIND, id, &fd);
    g_assert_cmpint(fd, >=, 0);
    size = m.a;
    g_assert_cmpuint(size, >=, VAS_HEADER_SIZE + FH + TH);
    shm = mmap(NULL, size, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_SHARED, fd, 0);
    g_assert(shm != MAP_FAILED);
    close(fd);
    g_assert_cmpuint(vas_load(shm, VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_SIZE), ==, TH);

    /* The doorbell becomes a KICK; the bytes themselves are already in renderd's view. */
    send_msg(&va, a, id, "hello");
    expect_msg(&fr, KRD_KICK, id, NULL);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_read(shm, size, got, sizeof(got)), ==, 5);
    g_assert_cmpstr(got, ==, "hello");

    /* Renderd writes a reply into the ring and asks for the pin: QEMU queues it in BAR1. */
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_write(shm, size, "world", 5), ==, 5);
    m = (KrdMsg){ .type = KRD_RAISE, .stream = id };
    g_assert_cmpint(krd_send(fr.conn, &m, -1, 0), ==, 0);
    for (i = 0; i < 2000 && qpci_io_readl(va.dev, va.bar1, 0x20) == 0; i++) {
        g_usleep(1000);
    }
    g_assert_cmpuint(qpci_io_readl(va.dev, va.bar1, 0x20), ==, 1);
    g_assert_cmpuint(qpci_io_readb(va.dev, va.bar1, 0), ==, ctz32(pin));
    expect_echo(&va, a, 0, "world");

    /* Ring growth: the stream moves to a new block, which renderd gets as a new fd. */
    b = stream_block(&va);
    g_assert_cmpuint(bind_sd(&va, b, a, 0x6701, &pin2), ==, id);
    expect_msg(&fr, KRD_REBIND, id, &fd);
    g_assert_cmpint(fd, >=, 0);
    close(fd);
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 1, a), ==, 0); /* renderd's mapping keeps its memory; QEMU's goes */
    g_assert_cmpuint(vas_load(shm, VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_SIZE), ==, TH);

    /* TERMINATE becomes UNBIND. */
    g_assert_cmpuint(by_offset(&va, 8, b), ==, 0);
    expect_msg(&fr, KRD_UNBIND, id, NULL);

    /* Renderd going away leaves QEMU running; new streams are refused. */
    close(fr.conn);
    for (i = 0; i < 2000 && bind_sd(&va, b, b, 0x6701, &pin); i++) {
        by_offset(&va, 8, b);
        g_usleep(1000);
    }
    g_assert_cmpuint(bind_sd(&va, b, b, 0x6701, &pin), ==, 0);

    munmap(shm, size);
    dev_stop(&va);
    close(fr.lst);
    unlink(fr.path);
    g_free(fr.path);
}

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    g_test_init(&argc, &argv, NULL);
    qtest_add_func("/vaddress/commands", test_vaddress);
    qtest_add_func("/vaddress/echo", test_echo);
    qtest_add_func("/vaddress/renderd", test_renderd);
    return g_test_run();
}

```

`source/qemu/tests/unit/test-vaddress-ring.c`:

```c
/*
 * Unit test for the VAddressStream host ring code.
 * The guest side is modelled from kiln/docs/vaddress-guest.md section 6.
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
 */

#include "qemu/osdep.h"
#include "hw/misc/vaddress-ring.h"

#define FROM_HOST_SIZE 64
#define TO_HOST_SIZE   64
#define BLK_SIZE (VAS_HEADER_SIZE + FROM_HOST_SIZE + TO_HOST_SIZE)

static uint8_t blk[BLK_SIZE];

static void setup(void)
{
    memset(blk, 0, sizeof(blk));
    vas_store(blk, VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_DATA_OFFSET,
              VAS_HEADER_SIZE + FROM_HOST_SIZE);
    vas_store(blk, VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_SIZE, TO_HOST_SIZE);
    vas_store(blk, VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_DATA_OFFSET, VAS_HEADER_SIZE);
    vas_store(blk, VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_SIZE, FROM_HOST_SIZE);
}

/* Guest producer on the to-host ring (split writes, as writeFullyImpl). */
static size_t guest_write(const uint8_t *buf, size_t len)
{
    size_t d = VAS_TO_HOST, total = 0;
    uint8_t *data = blk + VAS_HEADER_SIZE + FROM_HOST_SIZE;
    uint32_t wp = vas_load(blk, d + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS);
    uint32_t rp = vas_load(blk, d + VAS_RING_READ_POS);

    while (total < len) {
        uint32_t room = wp < rp ? rp - wp - 1 : TO_HOST_SIZE - wp - (rp == 0);
        size_t n = MIN(room, len - total);
        if (!n) {
            break;
        }
        memcpy(data + wp, buf + total, n);
        if (wp >= rp) {
            vas_store(blk, d + VAS_RING_WRAP_POS, wp + n);
        }
        wp = (wp + n) % TO_HOST_SIZE;
        total += n;
    }
    vas_store(blk, d + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS, wp);
    return total;
}

/* Guest consumer on the from-host ring. */
static size_t guest_read(uint8_t *buf, size_t len)
{
    size_t d = VAS_FROM_HOST, total = 0;
    uint8_t *data = blk + VAS_HEADER_SIZE;
    uint32_t wp = vas_load(blk, d + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS);
    uint32_t wrap = vas_load(blk, d + VAS_RING_WRAP_POS);
    uint32_t rp = vas_load(blk, d + VAS_RING_READ_POS);

    while (total < len && rp != wp) {
        uint32_t end = wp >= rp ? wp : wrap;
        size_t n = MIN(end - rp, len - total);
        memcpy(buf + total, data + rp, n);
        total += n;
        rp += n;
        if (wp < rp && rp == wrap) {
            rp = 0;
        }
    }
    vas_store(blk, d + VAS_RING_READ_POS, rp);
    return total;
}

/* Push a long pattern through a ring in odd-sized pieces; check the order. */
static void stream(size_t (*put)(const uint8_t *, size_t),
                   size_t (*get)(uint8_t *, size_t))
{
    uint8_t in[4096], out[4096];
    size_t sent = 0, got = 0, step = 0;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(in); i++) {
        in[i] = i * 31 + 7;
    }
    while (got < sizeof(in)) {
        size_t w = 1 + (step * 7) % 50, r = 1 + (step * 13) % 45;
        sent += put(in + sent, MIN(w, sizeof(in) - sent));
        got += get(out + got, r);
        g_assert_cmpuint(got, <=, sent);
        g_assert(++step < 100000);
    }
    g_assert_cmpmem(in, sizeof(in), out, sizeof(out));
}

static size_t host_read(uint8_t *buf, size_t len)
{
    ssize_t n = vas_ring_read(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, len);
    g_assert_cmpint(n, >=, 0);
    return n;
}

static size_t host_write(const uint8_t *buf, size_t len)
{
    ssize_t n = vas_ring_write(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, len);
    g_assert_cmpint(n, >=, 0);
    return n;
}

static void test_to_host(void)
{
    setup();
    stream(guest_write, host_read);
}

static void test_from_host(void)
{
    setup();
    stream(host_write, guest_read);
}

static void test_never_overfills(void)
{
    uint8_t buf[256] = { 0 };

    setup();
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_room(blk, BLK_SIZE), ==, FROM_HOST_SIZE - 1);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_write(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, sizeof(buf)), ==,
                    FROM_HOST_SIZE - 1);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_room(blk, BLK_SIZE), ==, 0);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_write(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, 1), ==, 0);
    /* reader frees 10 bytes, writer wraps into them */
    vas_store(blk, VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_READ_POS, 10);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_room(blk, BLK_SIZE), ==, 10);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_write(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, sizeof(buf)), ==, 10);
}

static void test_rejects_bad_header(void)
{
    uint8_t buf[8];

    /* ring past the end of the block */
    setup();
    vas_store(blk, VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_SIZE, BLK_SIZE);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_read(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, 8), ==, -1);
    /* data offset inside the header page */
    setup();
    vas_store(blk, VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_DATA_OFFSET, 0x10);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_write(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, 8), ==, -1);
    /* positions out of range, including a huge wrap_pos */
    setup();
    vas_store(blk, VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_WRITE_POS, TO_HOST_SIZE);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_read(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, 8), ==, -1);
    setup();
    vas_store(blk, VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_READ_POS, 8);
    vas_store(blk, VAS_TO_HOST + VAS_RING_WRAP_POS, 0xffffffff);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_read(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, 8), ==, -1);
    setup();
    vas_store(blk, VAS_FROM_HOST + VAS_RING_READ_POS, 0x80000000);
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_write(blk, BLK_SIZE, buf, 8), ==, -1);
    /* block smaller than the header */
    setup();
    g_assert_cmpint(vas_ring_read(blk, 0x800, buf, 8), ==, -1);
}

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    g_test_init(&argc, &argv, NULL);
    g_test_add_func("/vaddress-ring/to-host", test_to_host);
    g_test_add_func("/vaddress-ring/from-host", test_from_host);
    g_test_add_func("/vaddress-ring/never-overfills", test_never_overfills);
    g_test_add_func("/vaddress-ring/rejects-bad-header",
                    test_rejects_bad_header);
    return g_test_run();
}

```

`source/scripts/apply-qemu.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Copy the kiln QEMU sources into a QEMU tree and hook them into the build.
# Idempotent. Usage: scripts/apply-qemu.sh <qemu_src_dir>
set -eu

Q="${1:?usage: $0 <qemu_src_dir>}"
O="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)/qemu"

# Same relative layout as the QEMU tree.
(cd "$O" && find . -type f -name '*.[ch]') | while read -r f; do
  mkdir -p "$Q/$(dirname "$f")"
  cp "$O/$f" "$Q/$f"
done

# Changes to QEMU's own files (qemu/patches/*.patch, -p1): applied once, a patch that is already in is skipped.
for p in "$O"/patches/*.patch; do
  [ -e "$p" ] || continue
  if (cd "$Q" && patch -p1 -R --dry-run -s -f < "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1); then continue; fi
  (cd "$Q" && patch -p1 -s < "$p")
done

grep -q "config VADDRESS" "$Q/hw/misc/Kconfig" || cat >> "$Q/hw/misc/Kconfig" <<'K'

config VADDRESS
    bool
    default y if PCI_DEVICES
    depends on PCI
K
grep -q "vaddress-gl.c" "$Q/hw/misc/meson.build" || {
  sed -i '' "/CONFIG_VADDRESS/d" "$Q/hw/misc/meson.build"
  echo "system_ss.add(when: 'CONFIG_VADDRESS', if_true: files('vaddress.c', 'vaddress-echo.c', 'vaddress-gl.c'))" >> "$Q/hw/misc/meson.build"
}
# The 0x9800 service sub-device was removed: drop it from an already patched tree.
rm -f "$Q/hw/misc/vaddress-service.c"
sed -i '' "s/ 'vaddress-service.c',//" "$Q/hw/misc/meson.build"
grep -q "vaddress-test" "$Q/tests/qtest/meson.build" ||
  sed -i '' "s/^qtests_aarch64 = \\\\\$/qtests_aarch64 = \\\\\n  (config_all_devices.has_key('CONFIG_VADDRESS') and config_all_devices.has_key('CONFIG_ARM_VIRT') ? ['vaddress-test'] : []) + \\\\/" "$Q/tests/qtest/meson.build"
grep -q "test-vaddress-ring" "$Q/tests/unit/meson.build" ||
  sed -i '' "s/^  'test-int128': \[\],\$/  'test-int128': [],\n  # all code tested by test-vaddress-ring is inside vaddress-ring.h\n  'test-vaddress-ring': [],/" "$Q/tests/unit/meson.build"
grep -q "vaddress-test" "$Q/tests/qtest/meson.build"
grep -q "test-vaddress-ring" "$Q/tests/unit/meson.build"

```

`source/scripts/build-qemu.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/bash
# Builds QEMU as it ships in Kiln's runtime, for the oldest macOS Kiln supports (MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET, the app's
# LSMinimumSystemVersion). Homebrew's bottles and a plain build are made for the Mac they are built on, so a runtime bundled
# from them only starts on that macOS or later.
#
# 1. The libraries QEMU links, from source, into $PREFIX (default $DEV/kiln-deps; sources and build trees in $PREFIX/src):
#    glib (with pcre2 and proxy-libintl from its own subprojects), pixman, libslirp, zstd, SDL3 and sdl2-compat. One already
#    installed is skipped (delete $PREFIX to rebuild them).
# 2. QEMU in $DEV/qemu-kiln/build-14 (sources already patched by scripts/apply-qemu.sh), against those libraries only,
#    without the features Kiln does not use (VNC, TLS, SSH, host USB, capstone, rutabaga, Apple's PVG).
#
# The build trees keep no record of the target: always build them through this script. Needs meson, ninja, cmake and
# pkg-config. The paths at the top are the build machine's: change DEV for yours.
#
# Usage: scripts/build-qemu.sh [prefix]
set -euo pipefail

export MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET=14.0
DEV=/Volumes/Expansion/Users/nks1608/Developer
PREFIX=${1:-$DEV/kiln-deps}
SRC=$PREFIX/src
mkdir -p "$SRC" "$PREFIX/lib"
# only this prefix and macOS: nothing from Homebrew may leak into a build
export PKG_CONFIG_LIBDIR=$PREFIX/lib/pkgconfig:$PREFIX/share/pkgconfig
export PKG_CONFIG_PATH=
export CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH=$PREFIX
JOBS=$(sysctl -n hw.ncpu)

# fetch <url> <dir name>: downloads and unpacks into $SRC/<dir name>
fetch() {
  local url=$1 dir=$SRC/$2
  [ -d "$dir" ] && return
  local tmp=$SRC/.unpack
  rm -rf "$tmp" && mkdir -p "$tmp"
  curl -fL --retry 3 -o "$tmp/archive" "$url"
  tar -xf "$tmp/archive" -C "$tmp"
  rm "$tmp/archive"
  mv "$tmp"/* "$dir"
  rm -rf "$tmp"
}

meson_build() {  # <dir> [options]
  local dir=$SRC/$1; shift
  rm -rf "$dir/build"
  meson setup "$dir/build" "$dir" --prefix="$PREFIX" --libdir=lib --buildtype=release -Ddefault_library=shared "$@"
  # meson finds pipe2 in the SDK (macOS 27 has it) although the deployment target is 14: the library would call a weak symbol
  # that is NULL there (it killed QEMU at start-up on macOS 14). Without HAVE_PIPE2 glib uses pipe() and fcntl().
  [ -f "$dir/build/config.h" ] && sed -i '' '/^#define HAVE_PIPE2 /d' "$dir/build/config.h"
  ninja -C "$dir/build" install
}

cmake_build() {  # <dir> [options]
  local dir=$SRC/$1; shift
  cmake -S "$dir" -B "$dir/build" -G Ninja -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$PREFIX" -DCMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR=lib \
    -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_OSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET=$MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET -DCMAKE_FIND_FRAMEWORK=LAST "$@"
  ninja -C "$dir/build" install
}

installed() { [ -e "$PREFIX/lib/$1" ] && echo "have $1"; }

installed libglib-2.0.dylib || {
  fetch https://download.gnome.org/sources/glib/2.90/glib-2.90.1.tar.xz glib
  # pcre2 and libintl from glib's own wraps; libffi and libiconv from macOS
  meson_build glib --force-fallback-for=pcre2,intl -Dtests=false -Dintrospection=disabled -Dman-pages=disabled \
    -Ddocumentation=false -Dnls=disabled
}

installed libpixman-1.dylib || {
  fetch https://cairographics.org/releases/pixman-0.46.4.tar.gz pixman
  meson_build pixman -Dtests=disabled -Ddemos=disabled -Dgtk=disabled -Dlibpng=disabled
}

installed libslirp.dylib || {
  fetch https://gitlab.freedesktop.org/slirp/libslirp/-/archive/v4.9.5/libslirp-v4.9.5.tar.gz libslirp
  meson_build libslirp
}

installed libzstd.dylib || {
  fetch https://github.com/facebook/zstd/archive/refs/tags/v1.5.7.tar.gz zstd
  cmake_build zstd/build/cmake -DZSTD_BUILD_PROGRAMS=OFF -DZSTD_BUILD_STATIC=OFF -DZSTD_BUILD_TESTS=OFF \
    -DZSTD_LZ4_SUPPORT=OFF -DZSTD_LZMA_SUPPORT=OFF -DZSTD_ZLIB_SUPPORT=OFF
}

installed libSDL3.dylib || {
  fetch https://github.com/libsdl-org/SDL/releases/download/release-3.4.18/SDL3-3.4.18.tar.gz SDL3
  cmake_build SDL3 -DSDL_TESTS=OFF -DSDL_EXAMPLES=OFF -DSDL_STATIC=OFF -DSDL_X11=OFF
}

installed libSDL2-2.0.dylib || installed libSDL2.dylib || {
  fetch https://github.com/libsdl-org/sdl2-compat/releases/download/release-2.32.74/sdl2-compat-2.32.74.tar.gz sdl2-compat
  cmake_build sdl2-compat -DSDL2COMPAT_TESTS=OFF -DSDL2COMPAT_STATIC=OFF
}

Q=$DEV/qemu-kiln
[ -f "$Q/build-14/build.ninja" ] || (mkdir -p "$Q/build-14" && cd "$Q/build-14" && ../configure \
  --python=/opt/homebrew/bin/python3.14 --target-list=aarch64-softmmu --enable-hvf --disable-docs --enable-sdl \
  --enable-slirp --enable-zstd --disable-pvg --disable-capstone --disable-gnutls --disable-nettle --disable-gcrypt \
  --disable-png --disable-vnc --disable-libssh --disable-libusb --disable-rutabaga-gfx --disable-gtk --disable-curl \
  --disable-curses --disable-libudev --disable-sdl-image --disable-virglrenderer --disable-opengl --disable-spice \
  --disable-lzo --disable-snappy --disable-bzip2 --disable-lzfse --disable-libnfs --disable-libiscsi --disable-guest-agent)
ninja -C "$Q/build-14"  # all: the binaries, the firmware and keymaps bundle-runtime.py copies

echo "QEMU built for macOS $MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET"

```
